# Progress

This log keeps the current month. Earlier months rotate into `memory-bank/archive/` on the first session of each month; May to August 2026 is in [the progress archive](archive/progress-2026-05-to-08.md).

## Clipped Slayer text now fails the test lanes — 2026-09-27

The Slayer completion dialog and the "Slayer: 4 of 17 left" line in the review HUD are now checked for clipped, escaping or overlapping text in every offscreen Qt run, at the default 1000x700 parent and at a 900x560 laptop parent. Before, only `tools/pass14/screenshots.py` held these checks, and no test runner called it, so clipped Slayer text passed every gate (triage T6, decision 12 "a new check becomes a test"). The player sees no change.

`tests/ui_flows/qt_text_fit.py` `text_fit_defects(root)` names each visible label or button text that leaves the root, overlaps another, or does not fit its widget: `horizontalAdvance` and line spacing for plain labels, `heightForWidth` plus the longest-word `minimumSizeHint` width for wrapped labels, `sizeHint` width for buttons. `test_dialog_lifecycle.DialogLifecycleTest.test_slayer_completion_dialog_uses_the_level_up_surface` and the new `test_slayer_progress.SlayerProgressTextFitTest.test_combat_hud_keeps_every_text_whole_in_the_strip_at_both_parent_sizes` (on the `ReviewStripTest` reviewer harness, which also asserts the HUD stays inside the strip and the parent) call it after show, resize and four event passes. `tools/pass14/screenshots.py` is deleted, so no second copy of the check survives.

Red, with temporary mutations `message_label.setFixedWidth(80)` in `ui/completion_dialogs.py` and `self.slayer_lbl.setFixedWidth(40)` in `ReviewHUD`: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt --select test_slayer_completion_dialog_uses_the_level_up_surface --select test_slayer_progress --expected-tests 3` → `Ran 3 tests in 0.210s`, `FAILED (failures=4)`, exit 1, with `'You have defeated 17 bears. See a Slayer master for a new assignment.' is clipped in PyQt6.QtCore.QSize(80, 78)` and `'Slayer: 4 of 17 left' is clipped in PyQt6.QtCore.QSize(40, 14)` at both parent sizes (`red-mutated-80px-qt.log`, SHA-256 `ba482d389c9502f9b7b04a97d0f3b07b0c61e267e2c743cae1b3cce152259ab6`). An earlier 120 px dialog mutation still fitted the wrapped message inside the fixed 380x200 dialog, and that dialog subtest correctly passed (`red-mutated-120px-qt.log`). Both mutations were reverted with `git checkout -- ui/completion_dialogs.py ui/ui.py` before any other step.

## The finished tier-package migration can no longer rewrite the repository — 2026-09-27

A tool user can no longer run the completed 1,052-line tier-package migration by mistake; its default run (without `--dry-run`) still did `git mv` and rewrote imports across the repository. The utilities kept for manual use now have one caller record, `techContext.md` "Retained standalone utilities", with a supported command for each.

Caller search on main `53839d26`: `git grep -n -I` for the script's file name found one hit, the script's own self-exclusion check at its line 924; a phrase search (`tier_packages`, `tier migration`, `tier-package migration`) added only `memory-bank/papercuts-fixed-2026-09-23.md:1682`, a historical `git log -S` note about the migration commit, which stays. The `AnkiScape tier package: <name>` docstrings in the seven package `__init__.py` files (`core`, `data`, `generated`, `logic`, `logic_pure`, `runtime`, `ui`) are the migration's output, not callers. No test or pin counts tool files (`tests/test_module_surface.py` globs only `core/constants_parts`). After deletion, `git grep -n` for the file name and a `grep -rIn --exclude-dir=.git` of the worktree, untracked and ignored files included, both exit 1 with no output; this entry names the script only in prose so that search stays clean.

The whole-word stem scan of all tracked text files found 48 tool scripts with no reference outside themselves: the migration, 24 asset fetchers, 20 renderers, the Dominion Tower harvester and the two retained combat comparators (the audit of `d0904603` counted 21 renderers). None was deleted on a zero count; all 47 kept scripts are listed with their commands, and none has an unclear purpose. Each fetcher, the harvester and both comparators exit 0 on `--help` (27 of 27). Each renderer was run once with `.venv-qt/bin/python` into `/tmp`: 18 of 20 exit 0 and write their PNGs.

Proof on the MacBook: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --select test_docs_audit. --expected-tests 54` → `Ran 54 tests in 63.852s`, `OK`, exit 0 (rerun after this entry was written). `tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt` with the eight `test_module_surface` tests other than `test_root_initializer_is_reported_as_unavailable_without_aqt`, `--expected-tests 8` → `Ran 8 tests in 19.102s`, `OK`, exit 0. No red run exists for a pure deletion. Last remote run, the full plain lane: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain` → `Ran 4926 tests in 410.465s`, `OK (skipped=531)`, exit 0 (`full-plain.log` SHA-256 `028f1a7932ac1152b780caf3f51df707312cb4cd9d68e8eb67c8eb7f3d708c17`); only this entry's result lines changed after it. ## A queued remote test run tests the tree as launched — 2026-09-25

A focused or full run that waits for the MacBook mirror lock now tests the worktree as it stood when the run started, so stashing or editing while the run queues no longer changes what gets tested.

Verified claim, on `main` `d0904603`: `tools/run_remote_tests.py` `main()` entered `_hold_mirror_lock()` (untimed blocking `fcntl.flock(..., LOCK_EX)`) and only then called `_sync(local_worktree)`, whose `_rsync_command` copies the live `f"{local_worktree}/"`. Fix: `main()` copies the worktree into a `tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="ankiscape-remote-sync-")` with the same `_rsync_command` exclusions inside the "Remote preparation" phase, before the preflight and the lock wait, and syncs that snapshot; the snapshot is removed when the run ends. Lane acceptance, floors, the mirror lock, the inherited lock of the promotion gate and the direct `_sync` callers are unchanged. A local snapshot of this 73 MB worktree measured 2.26 to 2.50 s.

Proof on the MacBook: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_remote_test_host. --select test_remote_mirror_lock. --expected-tests 26` → `Ran 26 tests in 7.109s`, `OK`, exit 0; out-of-tree `focused-plain-runner.log` SHA-256 `0688322167ef7af7bb4677db81ad385cfdffa1915fa8fa1e4b7a57cd833c8731`. The regression test simulates contention: it patches `fcntl.flock` so the non-blocking attempt raises `BlockingIOError` and the blocking call edits, deletes and adds worktree files before returning; it proves the snapshot is taken before the lock wait, not behaviour under a real operating-system lock wait, so commit `13f2deb2`'s phrase "during a real lock wait" overstates it. Load-bearing check: with `_sync(Path(snapshot))` temporarily changed back to `_sync(local_worktree)`, `--select test_run_syncs_the_worktree_as_launched_not_as_edited_during_the_lock_wait --expected-tests 1` → `FAILED (failures=1)`, `AssertionError: {'run[31 chars]y': 'edited while queued\n', 'added.py': 'edit[14 chars]d\n'} != {'run[31 chars]y': 'launch\n', 'removed.py': 'launch\n'}`, exit 1; out-of-tree `mutation-live-tree-sync.log` SHA-256 `52d494c96f2a4e604e9742ad0161dadd456d417f2eeaba0e6bbb4b7e7372b704`; the change was restored before any other step.

Proof on the MacBook: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_remote_test_host. --select test_remote_mirror_lock. --expected-tests 26` → `Ran 26 tests in 7.109s`, `OK`, exit 0; out-of-tree `focused-plain-runner.log` SHA-256 `0688322167ef7af7bb4677db81ad385cfdffa1915fa8fa1e4b7a57cd833c8731`. Load-bearing check: with `_sync(Path(snapshot))` temporarily changed back to `_sync(local_worktree)`, `--select test_run_syncs_the_worktree_as_launched_not_as_edited_during_the_lock_wait --expected-tests 1` → `FAILED (failures=1)`, `AssertionError: {'run[31 chars]y': 'edited while queued\n', 'added.py': 'edit[14 chars]d\n'} != {'run[31 chars]y': 'launch\n', 'removed.py': 'launch\n'}`, exit 1; out-of-tree `mutation-live-tree-sync.log` SHA-256 `52d494c96f2a4e604e9742ad0161dadd456d417f2eeaba0e6bbb4b7e7372b704`; the change was restored before any other step.

Audits: `python3 tools/audit_docs.py` → `Audited 871 Markdown and 452 HTML files.` / `Documentation audit passed.`, exit 0; `python3 tools/audit_test_quality.py` → `4880 tests across 284 files` / `hard issues: 0` / `structural clone candidates: 31 groups`, exit 0; `python3 tools/audit_item_reachability.py` → `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`, exit 0. ## Astral runes open with Lunar Diplomacy instead of an item that does not exist — 2026-09-27

A player who had finished Rune Mysteries and Lunar Diplomacy, had Runecrafting 40 and carried Pure essence saw Astral runes locked for "altar access". The access check built an "Astral talisman" and "Astral tiara" from the rune name, and no such items exist in the game, so only an Omni-talisman staff or an Omni-fed Wicked hood opened the row. The altar record `AltarDefinition("astral", "Astral altar", None, None, "Astral rune")` in `data/runecrafting_data.py` gives the Astral altar no talisman and no tiara, and `quest-unlock-audit-2026-06-17.md` gates it by Lunar Diplomacy alone.

The access check now reads each altar's talisman and tiara from `ALTARS_BY_ID`. An altar with neither is not item-gated and gives the same 28-essence base as a tiara, a hood or a talisman staff, because no key takes a slot. Every other altar keeps its access items. Lunar Diplomacy still locks the row through the existing quest gate. The name-derived fallback and its unused runecrafting-data parameter are removed.

Red (base code, final test): `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_astral_access --expected-tests 2` → `AssertionError: Tuples differ: ('altar access',) != ()` and `AssertionError: Tuples differ: ('Lunar Diplomacy', 'altar access') != ('Lunar Diplomacy',)`, `Ran 2 tests in 0.002s`, `FAILED (failures=2)`, exit 1. Green: the same command → `Ran 2 tests in 0.002s`, `OK`, exit 0. Neighbours: `--select test_logic. --select test_great_orb_project. --select test_wicked_hood_logic. --select test_runtime_actions. --select test_undo_seam_wickedhood. --select test_astral_access. --expected-tests 467` → `Ran 467 tests in 2.592s`, `OK`; `--select test_target_metadata. --expected-tests 58` → `Ran 58 tests in 0.308s`, `OK`. Logs are in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-astral-talisman-requirement-2026-09-27/`.

Memory Bank readers: `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` → `Ran 46 tests in 60.340s`, `OK`.

## A TzHaar Fight Pit loss counts as a failure — 2026-09-27

A lost Fight Pit match now shows in the pit's action record as one failure of kind `safe_death`, not as a success. Anyone reading the action record (the achievement seeding for old saves in `core/storage_pure.py`, the Gnome Restaurant cooked-target gate, or a developer reading `action_telemetry`) now sees the pit's losses. This matches Castle Wars and Dominion Tower, which already record a safe death as a failure. Tokkul, the win, loss and match counters, and the Karamja and Miscellaneous diary conditions (which read `tzhaar_fight_pit_matches` and `tzhaar_fight_pit_wins`) are unchanged. Because the shared `_record_action` gates achievement-ledger credit and quest action proofs on success, the losing beat no longer adds one to the `utility` achievement-ledger count for `tzhaar_fight_pit`, exactly as a Castle Wars or Dominion Tower death does not; no diary or quest reads that count. No screen changes.

Red, before the fix (MacBook): `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_runtime_loss_is_recorded_as_a_safe_death_failure --expected-tests 1` → `AssertionError: 1 != 0` at `self.assertEqual(record["successes"], 0)`, `FAILED (failures=1)`, exit 1. The test enters the pit at 1 lifepoint and drives the real `handle_tzhaar_fight_pit_answer` with a fixed 0.0 roll to a real loss; nothing is stubbed.

Green (MacBook): the same command → `Ran 1 test in 0.001s`, `OK`, exit 0. Neighbours: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_tzhaar_fight_pit. --select test_undo_seam_fightpit. --select test_write_through_tzhaar_fight_pit. --select test_runtime_actions. --select test_minigame_xp_settlement. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --expected-tests 266` → `Ran 266 tests in 16.351s`, `OK`, exit 0. ## A resumed or reused lane can no longer block later runs — 2026-09-27

Fourth review (Grok 4.7, `.orchestrator/grok-reviews-2026-09-27/runner-orphan-lane.md`) found two real gaps. The landed fix is the process-group scan in `tools/run_remote_tests.py`: the scan counted every process whose group id matched the recorded leader pid, so a later session leader that reused an exited leader's pid blocked reconciliation and could be signalled by a stop; because a real leader always holds the pid file and log on its standard streams, the scan now ignores the group when the process at that pid holds neither file. This replaces the review's suggested start-time record, with no pid-file format change. The other gap was the carried driver's resume, which raised its timeout, and returned a report, without stopping its lane; its fix in `observe_existing` and `run_job` was dropped because `ffb27b38` deleted `tools/carried/run_processing_reconciliation_remote.py`, the only resume path. The unbounded log and report polls are pre-existing and recorded as a papercut.

Raw logs: `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-runner-orphan-lane-2026-09-25/`.

- Not run: no test ran for this change. The one focused attempt at 2026-09-27T01:52:04-0700 exited 1 with `error: remote plain prerequisites failed with exit 255; stdout: ''; stderr: 'ArmanTarkhanian@armans-macbook-pro: Permission denied (publickey,password,keyboard-interactive).\n'` (`review5-focused-plain-attempt.log`), and local `lsof` hangs on this Mac, so the scan cannot be exercised here.
- Run after the rebuild on main `53839d26`: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_remote_test_host. --select test_remote_mirror_lock. --select test_gate_lock_claim. --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 85` (30 + 6 + 3 + 35 + 11 = 85, counted by `def test_`) → `Ran 85 tests in 83.578s`, `OK`, exit 0 (`goal-runner-orphan-lane-on-main-2026-09-27/focused-plain.log`). The new test is the reused-pid case; `test_remote_mirror_lock` includes main's waiting-gate test.
- Red run already made on the branch, with both fixes reverted: `Ran 31 tests in 17.382s`, `FAILED (failures=2, errors=1)` (`red-both-fixes-reverted.log`, SHA-256 `58197e7d6f6c9b20175fece6092aa81891a0c7ef14b8a6fdb9178b0fc93e2598`; the log does not record its command line). On main only the reused-pid error still applies; the carried-resume test was deleted with the driver.

## A lane check fails closed on the MacBook's own shell — 2026-09-26

Third review of this branch found four gaps. The process scan piped `ps` and `lsof` into `awk`, so a failed scan read as a stopped lane; each scanner's status now fails the check, and the probe holds both lane files itself, so a scan that does not list the probe fails too. Every `lsof -t` holder of the pid file counted as lane work, so a read-only inspector blocked syncs forever; a lane's processes are now those with its pid file or log open for writing as a standard stream, plus its group and descendants. `kill -"$signal" $processes` passed a newline-joined list as one pid under zsh, the MacBook's login shell; the stop now splits it with `xargs`, and the runner's execution tests run through `/bin/zsh -c`. Reconciliation had no deadline; its process-group read, liveness probe and report read now share one 90-second deadline, and an expired one keeps the record.

- Focused MacBook proof: `--select test_remote_test_host. --select test_remote_mirror_lock. --select test_gate_lock_claim. --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 83` → `Ran 83 tests in 90.059s`, `OK`, exit 0 on the committed code; `review4-focused-plain-final.log` SHA-256 `5eb892d6204c7cce4becaa7c1aa90061c5290b089735e93d36c8bc72b7d496e0`.
- Load-bearing proof: with the zsh-unsafe `kill`, a read-mode holder counted as lane work, the `ps` status ignored and a 3600-second reconciliation deadline, `--select test_remote_test_host. --expected-tests 29` → `Ran 29 tests in 166.283s`, `FAILED (failures=2, errors=3)`: both whole-lane timeout stops with `stop failed with exit 1`, the reader case, the failed-scan case and the stalled-reconciliation case (`review4-mutation-proof.log` SHA-256 `f188d186a342c541c4d9b5e5d27e0dde336556e9b18d14b1da46c9cb60e584a7`); `git diff | shasum` matched `788a72496b6a704dccbc189c432dbc13a39c3012` after restore.
- Memory Bank readers: `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` → `Ran 46 tests in 58.873s`, `OK`; `review4-memory-bank.log` SHA-256 `9e071cf02824c5d7048b722eb219f5066170d7870cce8a3a653ffbf0623e54cf`.

## A reported lane frees the mirror only once it is stopped — 2026-09-26

Second review of this branch found five gaps. A completion report cleared the pending record even while a detached helper still used the mirror, in both the poller and the next run's reconciliation; the poller now ends its own lane through `stop_pending_lane` after the report, and reconciliation clears a record that names a pid file only once its processes are confirmed gone. A record written by main's runner has no `pid_path` and was rejected as invalid, so a run in flight at landing blocked every later run; it now clears once its report exists, as before. The carried comparison driver's SSH wrapper rejected the new `deadline` keyword, so its timeout stop raised `TypeError`; it now forwards every option. After a confirmed timeout stop that driver's resume waited for a report that never comes; it now ends that run as a failed job with `exit_code` null and never relaunches it, and it clears a completed run's record through `stop_pending_lane`; `ffb27b38` deleted that driver, so these driver changes did not land. The unresolved-run test now allows only `cat` reads and the exact `LANE_LIVENESS` probe.

The previous follow-up's post-edit Memory Bank proof was `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` → `Ran 46 tests in 65.900s`, `OK`; `review-fix-memory-bank.log` SHA-256 `522755f57eb08f34e05a96ee90083fb3acd5bc3a743484429c82280ea4776a2f`.

## A timed-out test run stops only its own whole lane, within a deadline — 2026-09-26

Review of `85132cb1` found three gaps. The promotion gate stopped whatever lane held the pending record, so a runner killed before it launched could stop an earlier run's live orphan; the gate now stops a lane only when the record differs from the one present before that runner started. A lane was its process group only, so a test subprocess started in its own session survived the stop while the record was cleared; the pid file is now the lane's stdin, and a lane's processes are its group, every holder of that file (`/usr/sbin/lsof -t`) and all their descendants. The stop had no time bound over an established SSH session; `stop_pending_lane` now has a 90-second deadline on every SSH call, and an unconfirmed stop is logged, keeps the record and releases the lock. A subprocess that leaves the group, closes that stdin and outlives its parent still cannot be seen.

Raw logs: `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-runner-orphan-lane-2026-09-25/`.

- Load-bearing proof: with the record-identity check, the lsof holder scan, the descendant walk and the SSH deadline all disabled, `--select test_remote_test_host. --select test_remote_mirror_lock. --expected-tests 31` → `Ran 31 tests in 90.421s`, `FAILED (failures=4)`, exactly the four new or extended tests; the tree was restored and `git diff | shasum` matched the pre-mutation value. `review-fix-mutation-proof.log` SHA-256 `82f0e8cf9d0c45cceb9e76b7e4e63fe2cccc4b0b78f6b8b18010842a8ff15834`.

## An orphaned remote test lane no longer blocks every later run — 2026-09-25

A test run cut off at its time limit used to leave its MacBook lane running with a pending record, and every later run then failed at once with `previous remote lane has no completion report; sync blocked` until a human reconciled it (papercuts of 2026-09-23 name runs `qt-1790225120-677f3cf4`, `plain-1790230291-55e1095b` and `qt-1790231314-54789936`). Each lane now runs in its own MacBook process group, recorded in `reports/<run>.pid`. A runner that reaches its own `--timeout` stops that group, confirms it is gone and clears the record; a promotion gate whose lane limit kills the runner does the same while it still holds the mirror lock (the ec293a82 lock hold is unchanged). A later run still refuses to sync over a live group, and clears a record with a logged reconciliation once the lane has written its report or its group is confirmed gone. Group ids 0 and 1 and unreadable pid files are never signalled and keep blocking.

Branch `goal/runner-orphan-lane-2026-09-25` from main `d0904603`. Raw logs: `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-runner-orphan-lane-2026-09-25/`.

## Grand Exchange fills no longer send the player to the bank — 2026-09-22

The owner reported repeated "Bank run — Running to the bank · arrived and restocked" entries while cooking raw chicken. Their save held one buy offer for 100 Lobster at 228 coins, 76 filled in parts at cards 8518, 8520, 8523, 8524, 8525, 8526 and 8529. Each partial fill called the root trip trigger, which took the next admitted cooking action; the trip collected nothing, because offer items reach the bank only through Collect.

Market fills now trigger no trip. Placing an offer and collecting one still do. This narrows owner pass note 12 of `pass6-triage-2026-08-01.md` ("GE collection and buy/sell fills trigger a bank run"): the collection half stays, the fill half is removed. The old dispatcher split therefore cannot be promoted even though its historical lanes passed (§53.2 at `3172-3186`).

Disposition meanings: `eligible` has a settled action; `verification_pending` needs the unchanged gates before acceptance; `source_blocked` names missing evidence; `dependency_blocked` waits on a named live substrate; `integration_blocked` means a required host capability or fixed integration gate prevents acceptance; `excluded_ui` falls outside this goal; `closed` needs no implementation; `preserve` means do not merge, delete, or alter that work. A source-blocked unit does not stop independent eligible work.

## Reviewer HUD showed a bank run that never started — 2026-09-17

- Owner report at 23:07 PDT: Fishing `catch_tuna_swordfish` showed the HUD title `Bank run` with `Bank run queued` while every review still paid `+80 XP · Fishing`. The saved data held `skilling_bank_run_requested = "catch_mackerel_cod_bass"`, `current_fishing = "catch_tuna_swordfish"`, `skilling_bank_run = {}`, `carried_inventory["catch_tuna_swordfish"] = {"Raw tuna": 17}`, Fishing level 42 (swordfish needs 50, so tuna at 80 XP is the only eligible catch).
- Cause: a Grand Exchange fill or offer calls `_trigger_active_bank_run`, which records the active target in `skilling_bank_run_requested`. The carried overlay spends that request only on a review of the same target, but `review_bank_run_travel_view_pure` reported `queued` for any non-empty request. After a target switch the HUD showed a trip the runtime never ran.
- Fix: the travel view resolves the handler's current target through `review_handler_target_key` in `core/action_registry.py` and reports `queued` only when the request names that target, matching the runtime and the strip button. The request stays per-target by design (`test_bank_request_survives_reviews_on_another_surface` guards a quest review from waiving a mining trip). Regression test `tests/runtime_actions/test_bank_run_request_target.py` covers the HUD and the overlay together; 326 core tests and 45 Qt tests pass.
- The slop-gate refused a new `logic/review_bank_run_logic.py` module (`logic: 88 code files`, `tests: 237 code files`) and any growth of `ui/ui.py` (29,293 lines) or `__init__.py` fan-out, so the fix stays in `logic/review_strip_logic.py`, which shrank from 1,054 to 1,047 lines. One commit attempt ran complexipy for over 15 minutes before it reported.
- Second-level, not changed: the XP notification names the skill, never the catch (`+80 XP · Fishing`), so a spread target reads as a nondescript fish. Changing that copy needs owner approval.

## Truncated navigation follows settled menu decisions — 2026-09-17

Then `03ea285d`, "Remove the unapproved menu tab styling", removed the six-line block containing `setExpanding(True)` and `QTabBar::tab { min-width: 0px; padding-left: 5px; padding-right: 5px; }`. The existing record says `03ea285d` "restores the main menu to the owner-settled Qt behavior from `806a2f63`"; the UI and main-menu test files match exactly between those commits.
- Main `57e7a580` has no `setElideMode` in `ui/ui.py`, leaving elision to Qt's style default. Main-menu tabs at lines 4896-4898 have document mode and 18-pixel icons, without a local tab stylesheet. ## Union Alpha trial — 2026-09-16, Barcrawl slice

Thirteen union-alpha seats ran the Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl slice end to end,
landed on main at `3a14d435`. Scores for the routing rubric row follow.

### What it got right

- Followed an exact design faithfully. Every seat given a decided design
  implemented it without redesigning. The reachability extraction produced a
  byte-identical audit JSON, verified with `cmp` exit 0.
- Discovery quality was high. Four read-only mapping seats produced accurate
  `path:line` maps of the miniquest, persistence, user interface and vendor
  seams; spot checks confirmed names and content every time.
- Escalated rather than guessed. One seat refused to delete a test that
  encoded a forbidden design and asked for a ruling. Another verified a
  deletion by comparing abstract syntax trees, which the brief did not ask for.
- Self-corrected. Two seats retracted their own fabricated steering claims in
  their final reports without being challenged.
- Respected the rails. No seat weakened an assertion to pass, including when a
  test blocked its own fix.

### What it got wrong

- Fabricated steering. At least six seats opened reports with "Steer received:"
  describing a message that was never sent, then truncated their work. The
  explicit brief line "no steering has been sent to you and none will be" did
  NOT stop this; it changed the behaviour to fabricate-then-retract.
- Line citations drift. One seat transposed two ranges, placing
  `_on_state_did_undo` at 1409-1428 and `_restore_review_award_snapshot` at
  1477-1485; the real values are 1477 and 1418. Names and content were right.
- Declared a false blocker. One seat refused to implement, claiming the stage
  engine could not express order-independent collection. It can:
  `logic_pure/logic_pure_quest.py:787` loops every entry in `required_actions`
  and blocks until all are satisfied. That cost one idle iteration.
- Stalled on invented coordination. Two seats finished their work, then waited
  on a "background watcher" and a "gating review agent" that did not exist,
  and had to be told nothing was pending.
- Focused tests hid five real regressions. A seat reported its targeted modules
  green; the full lanes then found five failing tests it had never run.
- Hand-edited a generated file. Ten vendor rows went into `data/shop_data.py`,
  whose header forbids hand edits, leaving the file reporting
  `vendor_action_count: 4` while holding 14 rows. The next regeneration would
  have deleted the feature silently.

### Orchestrator errors in the same slice, for balance

- Specified ten separate collection stages, which made order-independence look
  impossible and triggered the false blocker above.
- Instructed a cross-quest proof gate on Scorpion Catcher, violating the
  invariant at `tests/test_quest_action_proofs.py`. Reverted in `2dcc9eee`.
- Recorded a lane-timeout blocker without testing the remote host first. The
  Qt lane needs 1712s on this Mac Studio and 1078s on the MacBook, against a
  1300s ceiling. The ceiling was correct; the host was wrong.

### Verdict for the rubric

Union Alpha is a competent implementation seat under a decided design, with
free cost and no effort parameter, provided the orchestrator verifies by
execution, runs both full lanes before calling anything done, and treats every
declared blocker and line citation as unverified. It is not a seat that can be
left to decide scope.

## Module surface verification harness — 2026-09-01

The repository now has one deterministic surface instrument for pure moves.
`tools/module_surface.py` accepts dotted imports or repository `.py` paths,
normalizes nested mappings, sets, sequences, dataclasses, classes, functions,
and constants, and emits a final `MODULE_SURFACE_SHA256:` line. Batch mode
prints one digest per module from isolated child interpreters. The first-wave
aggregators have pinned digests in `tests/test_module_surface.py` (nine at
first, 15 since the September splits); the root initializer is pinned under
offscreen Qt as `ROOT_QT_DIGEST` in that test, which moves with every root
change, and skips cleanly without `aqt`, while the Shop tab imports in the plain lane. Focused
tests pass, documentation and test-quality audits pass, and the worktree guard
reports a safe layout.

## 2026-09-23 — Native verify refuses while Anki runs (papercut triage)

`tools/native/session_guard.py verify` measured the owner's collection and could print `"unchanged": true` while an Anki process was still open and able to write it; on 2026-09-21 `guard-final-verify.stdout` did exactly that before the later post-quit verify reported `unchanged: false`. `verify` now calls the existing fail-closed `require_anki_stopped()` after reading the receipt and before measuring, so a running Anki (or a failed `pgrep` probe) exits 2 with no JSON result. ## 2026-09-24 — Promotion gate lanes no longer pay for the mirror queue

Gate `1fc26752` failed on 2026-09-23 with `TIMEOUT: exceeded 1000 seconds` and `TIMEOUT: exceeded 1300 seconds` because each remote lane's fixed limit also counted the runner's wait for `~/Library/Caches/ankiscape-remote-test-host.lock` behind focused runs.

Design choice: the gate itself takes the mirror lock before its remote lanes and holds it across both; each lane runner inherits the locked descriptor (`ANKISCAPE_INHERITED_MIRROR_LOCK_FD` plus `pass_fds`) and uses it instead of queueing. The lane limits (plain 1,000 s, Qt 1,300 s), the outer wall-clock guard and its `TIMEOUT: exceeded N seconds` message are untouched, so they now bound only preflight, sync and execution, exactly as before for a lane's own work. The rejected alternative, having the runner start the limit at lock acquisition, would widen the gate's outer guard by the wait and leave a hung rsync unbounded until the wider guard fired. The gate's wait is bounded separately by `REMOTE_MIRROR_LOCK_TIMEOUT = 3600.0` (longer than another gate's whole 2,300 s lane pair), refreshes the gate heartbeat every second while it waits, logs `held after N seconds` or `TIMEOUT: waited 3600 seconds for <path>`, and on timeout fails both lanes as `(mirror lock wait timed out)` without starting them. The runner accepts an inherited descriptor only if it names the lock file and a nonblocking flock on it succeeds, so the variable cannot jump the queue. Pending-run reconciliation, serial ownership through the one lock, and the floors are unchanged.

Proof, evidence in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-gate-lock-wait-2026-09-24/`:
- `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_remote_test_host --select test_promotion_gate --select test_gate_lock_claim --select test_remote_mirror_lock --expected-tests 62` exited 0 with `Ran 62 tests in 71.457s` / `OK` (`remote-plain-focused-4.log`, sha256 `c3b7f82fa6d049de948990b8d7a42142dd9d2496298ab1cd0873833556eca6ca`). The first commit attempt was refused by slop-gate (`_run_gate_checks 30 (was 15)`, `tests/test_promotion_gate.py: 1026 lines (was 935)`; `commit-hook.log`, `a8a305e8063b8fe83c0ee3fab8b05c77e265b9754c90a4b8e6fa14b0856ac8db`), so the check loop became `_run_check_sequence` and the timing tests moved to `tests/promotion/test_remote_mirror_lock.py`; the pre-move pass is `remote-plain-focused-3.log` (`752b5b8310ceff8593e8df2ecf240515bc19f9e40d4a510932058ce6bf72e820`).

## 2026-09-24 — Qt lane speed: same tests, about a third less time

The offscreen Qt lane was slow for three test-side reasons, found by per-test timing on the MacBook. (1) Main-menu tests left their windows alive: tests clear `_captured`, and panels detached with `setParent(None)` outlive their dialog, so about 47,000 widgets stayed live after one pass of `test_main_menu_widget`. Every later app-wide `setStyle("Fusion")` or palette change repolished them (10 to 15 s per `setUpClass` in the Temple Trekking, Mobilising Armies and Gnome Restaurant panels, and 9 to 29 s per dark-mode main-menu test). `MainMenuWidgetTest.tearDown` now deletes every top-level window the test created, parented or not; a probe then found 0 leaked widgets after the module. (2) 23 unpatched `build_reachability_report()` calls across six modules each rebuilt the same live report (about 8.6 s each). `tests/reachability/live_report.py` builds it once per process and hands each caller a deep copy (2 ms); every test that patches game data still calls `build_reachability_report()` itself. (3) `setUp` built a default Mining dialog that 380 of the 421 main-menu tests never use; `dialog` is now a `cached_property` that builds it on first use. No product code, test, assertion, skip, limit or floor changed.

Discovered test ids are identical before and after (`run_tests.py --list` on both lanes: 5730 at a45c92d8 and on this tree, `cmp` of the sorted id lists exit 0); the brief's 5,731 does not match a45c92d8. Full Qt lane through `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py <tree> qt`: unchanged a45c92d8 export `/tmp/qtspeed-head`, `Ran 5730 tests in 1160.597s`, `OK (skipped=5)` (load 36.91 at 00:22, 9.53 at 00:48); this tree, `Ran 5730 tests in 764.065s`, `OK (skipped=5)` (load 11.53 at 00:04, 12.76 at 00:21).

## 2026-09-23 — Diary generator: condition keys and regional rosters simplified

`tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py` was 1,518 lines, over the 1,000-line limit that the split contract applies to every module it leaves. Two cohesive seams now live in `tools/achievement_diary_generation/`: `condition_keys.py` (309 lines) checks that each condition key names state the runtime writes, and `task_conditions.py` (569 lines) holds the curated override tables and the rules that turn a harvested task into its deed and access gates. The generator keeps roster curation, equipment rows and catalog layout at 722 lines. `resolve_condition_key` now looks each condition kind up in a table with one resolver per kind; the counter and `state_equals` paths share one default-save walk. `_regional_roster` now runs four named steps: rank each source tier, require sourced anchors, borrow the nearest unused deeds, and refuse duplicated anchors. The generator path re-exports every name it published before through plain imports marked `# noqa: F401`, with no `__all__`, so `from tools.generate_achievement_diary_data import *` still yields the same 38 names as on main, `build` and `FAMILIES` included.

Surface: `python3 tools/module_surface.py tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py` gives `MODULE_SURFACE_SHA256: 306df2874c7072a6d2ae5c0a0b406ff49599692a4959b85b10380260d5333bc2` before and after; the two dumps are byte-identical (dump SHA-256 `2861962d7ed07615a840c74ed132063c95a93b8564fca8d747c3259e2997d64b`).

## 2026-09-24 — Diary generator: the last five over-limit functions simplified

Five functions in the diary generator family were above the cognitive limit of 15. Each now reads as named steps. `build` renders each regional family through `_regional_family_block` and each single-pool family through `_special_family_block`, and then merges them in the same order. `_equipment_rows` reads a reward item's items.yml row through `_items_yml_equipment`. `_module_level_strings` and `_emitted_ledger_actions` share `_parsed_sources`, and they read their syntax trees through `_string_assignments` and `_ledger_write_calls`. `_condition_src` hands the `any_of` body to `_alternatives_src`. The generator is 720 lines, was 722. `MODULE_SURFACE_SHA256` is unchanged for all three modules (`306df287…`, `367692b1…`, `56115e66…`; the before and after dumps are byte-identical), and `from tools.generate_achievement_diary_data import *` still gives 38 names.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty), before → after: `_equipment_rows` 10/27/2.25 → 5/9/0.5 (`_items_yml_equipment` 6/5/1.75); `build` 11/24/2.5 → 5/2/0.5 (`_regional_family_block` 6/10/1.71, `_special_family_block` 4/3/1.12); `_module_level_strings` 11/23/1.5 → 5/5/0.5 (`_parsed_sources` 3/3/0, `_string_assignments` 7/6/1.0); `_emitted_ledger_actions` 11/24/2.5 → 7/9/0.5 (`_ledger_write_calls` 6/8/2.0); `_condition_src` 14/16/3.83 → 9/10/2.18 (`_alternatives_src` 5/4/1.0). complexipy `-mx 15` on the three files reports no failed function; the highest Halstead difficulty in them is 3.33.

Output identity: `python3 tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py memory-bank/source-audits/achievement-diary-harvest-2026-07-26.json --items-yml /Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Downloads/game-main/data/cfg/items.yml --out /tmp/diary-rest-{before,after}/data/achievement_diary_catalog.py`. `diff -r` finds the two trees identical. It finds `_module_level_strings` equal (938 names), `_emitted_ledger_actions` equal (22 skills, 2075 actions), and `_equipment_rows` equal on the real and synthetic items.yml. The missing-row and unmapped-slot refusals are identical, and `_condition_src` gives 0 mismatches over 159 conditions (every override plus six synthetic cases). Four of the six are refusals in both versions; the other two, "Bad key" and "Unresolvable", use the unknown counter `no_such_counter_xyz`, and both versions accept and serialise them, which is the gap recorded below.

Found only through this work, not fixed: a one-segment `counter` key that no named or special counter claims falls through to `_resolve_save_path`, which accepts it as "an absent per-key leaf". So `("counter", "no_such_counter_xyz", 1, …)` serialises without a refusal, and a typo in a top-level counter name would ship as a row that can never complete.

Both module surfaces are unchanged (`MODULE_SURFACE_SHA256` `d12a75aa…` for `contract.py`, `b7960e7f…` for `test_roster_contract.py`, equal to the pins in `tests/test_module_surface.py`).

## 2026-09-24 — Diary generator refuses a counter the game never counts

A one-segment `counter` key that no named or special counter claims used to fall through to `_resolve_save_path`, which accepted any absent final segment as "a per-key count not yet written". At the save root that reasoning is wrong: the root has a fixed shape, so `("counter", "no_such_counter_xyz", 1, …)` read zero forever and still serialised. `_resolve_save_path` now keeps the zero-case allowance only below the root, so an absent top-level name is refused for `counter` and, through the same walk, for `state_equals`. A `counter` path that exists but ends on a value that is not an `int` or `float` (for example `inventory`, a dict) is refused too, because the runtime's `_int` reads it as zero on every check; `bool` passes as an `int`, which keeps the shipped `house_owned` flag. The generator's refusal now also names the task id: `unresolvable diary condition in ardougne_easy_boot_camp (Bad key): counter key 'no_such_counter_xyz' has no 'no_such_counter_xyz' in a default save`.

Shipped content: a probe over the shipped catalog (803 conditions with alternatives and access gates, 132 `counter` and 9 `state_equals`) finds 19 distinct one-segment keys, all present in a default save, and 0 keys whose first segment is absent from it (probe SHA-256 `90dc15a2ec5c664d0ddb4c3b1dc3a688bbd24444eb6bf87a3181d4c130965004`). Every shipped `counter` that reaches the save walk ends on an `int` (45) or a `bool` (1, `house_owned`) (probe SHA-256 `b53aa011d0244b4d3bb64bcf6d34063972ec5fa7cc44b523b5592132bdb6a30f`). No shipped task is refused. The resolver probe prints `''` on HEAD and a refusal in the worktree for `no_such_counter_xyz` and `state_equals` `no_such_state`, and `''` in both for `castle_wars_wins` (SHA-256 `488853f0a05f5bfffd617f64a1355876378a306994ff8243c997c68731d941bd`); a second probe refuses `inventory` with `counter key 'inventory' holds a dict, not a count, in a default save` and passes `house_owned` (SHA-256 `d0aaf7806d526d88b34043c64abbfa7710aee11621992892662d0f0eb599752d`).

Output identity: `python3 tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py memory-bank/source-audits/achievement-diary-harvest-2026-07-26.json --items-yml /Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Downloads/game-main/data/cfg/items.yml --out /tmp/diary-counter-key-{before,after}/data/achievement_diary_catalog.py`; `diff -r` finds the two trees identical, and the 13-file manifest has SHA-256 `06434752e309f79186855049947882711b39914321324de89a180e994614825d` before and after, with 13 `OK` from `shasum -a 256 -c` against the checked-in files; a rerun with the non-count rule (`/tmp/diary-counter-key-review`) is identical too.

Focused MacBook proof: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_achievement_diaries. --select test_achievement_diaries_integration --select CurationLedgerWiringTest --select test_catalog_scan --select test_ledger_text` plus the 14 unskipped `CurationLedgerRuntimeExecutionTest` methods by name, `--expected-tests 163`, exited 0: `Ran 163 tests in 14.291s`, `OK` (log SHA-256 `1d73be4f780e55ecaf9e25b7c3cce3a0ebd98e2d402b3c8fd35e5ab6dd2736b5`). The papercuts reader `--select test_promotion_docs_explain_tree_owned_reachability_pins --expected-tests 1` exited 0: `Ran 1 test in 0.006s`, `OK` (log SHA-256 `873bc8b5d435efc53166c78a90e2a6256cb1e036c62d828b65b89cc89e2b2e90`). New test: `achievement_diaries.test_condition_keys.ConditionKeyRefusalTest.test_generator_refuses_a_top_level_counter_that_never_counts` drives `_task_block` with the made-up key and with `inventory`; it lives in its own module because the slop-gate pre-commit hook refuses growth of `tests/test_achievement_diary_ledger_wiring.py` (1054 lines). After that move, the same diary selection plus `--select test_condition_keys --select test_promotion_gate --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet`, `--expected-tests 209`, exited 0: `Ran 209 tests in 75.391s`, `OK` (log SHA-256 `37cab570058baad9571b48df464a420061c277e933a22c21138b84bfa7a88a6b`). ## 2026-09-26 — Retired checks cannot copy later edits into a remote run

The caller search covered tools, tests, briefs, plans and other tracked records. `tools/pass14/checks.py` had no executable caller. The only caller of `tools/carried/run_processing_reconciliation_remote.py` was its own unused canary, `tools/carried/check_processing_remote_resume.py`. Other references describe finished work or retained historical evidence. Deleted these runners and the canary instead of adding another snapshot path. The remaining tool call to `_sync` is `tools/run_remote_tests.py:607`, which supplies `Path(snapshot)`.

All Pass 14 selected modules still exist in test discovery. The ordinary module-surface tests retain the pinned checks. The carried pure checks, real recipe tests and remote-runner resumption tests remain unchanged. Added behavior tests for exact `False` rejection versus `None`/`0`, deferred callbacks, Winter Storage rollback, child cadence and advanced refills. These use the retained comparison tool's scenarios and the real carried wrapper; the historical comparator, its source guards and its other callers remain intact. The evidence summary now names the comparator as the reader of the retained source manifest.

MacBook proof after retirement: the carried suites, original recipe selectors and remote-runner suites passed `Ran 64 tests in 7.634s` / `OK`, exit 0, with `--expected-tests 64`. The initial conversion had `Ran 8 tests in 0.005s` / `FAILED (failures=2)`: rejected actions retain the legacy `Zero: 0` entry. ## 2026-09-25 — A candidate that changes the gate is judged by its own gate

The promotion gate judged every candidate with the gate code of the checkout that started it, normally the canonical one. When a batch changed what the gate expects, the old code rejected correct work: gate `1fc26752` failed reachability with `FAILED: exit 0` because the canonical gate still expected exit 1 while the batch contained `5fc1d538`. The only guard was the opt-in `own-gate` argument of `.orchestrator/gate_when_idle.sh`. `promotion_gate.py run` now compares the files the gate process itself imports (`GATE_SOURCES` in `tools/promotion/cli.py`: `promotion_gate.py`, `run_remote_tests.py`, `promotion/*.py`, `reachability/*.py` under `tools/`) byte for byte with the candidate's copies and, when they differ, prints `Candidate changes the gate; running <candidate>/tools/promotion_gate.py` and re-executes that script with the same arguments. The triage proposed `git diff --quiet <gate HEAD> <candidate> -- tools/promotion_gate.py tools/promotion`; comparing files on disk also covers `tools/reachability/pins.py` and `tools/run_remote_tests.py`, which the gate imports in-process, and needs no shared object store. Landing already accepted evidence from gate runs on the candidate's own gate code, so this adds no new trust. `gate_when_idle.sh` is outside the repository and still carries its `own-gate` option; it is now redundant.

Proof: `tests/promotion/test_candidate_gate.py` runs the real gate script against a candidate whose only change is two lines appended to `tools/promotion/lane_execution.py`, and the candidate's code answers. With the `cli.py` change reverted, the same test failed with `AssertionError: 2 != 0 : error: fatal: not a git repository (or any of the parent directories): .git` (`Ran 1 test in 0.099s`, `FAILED (failures=1)`); the file was restored before anything else. `test_remote_lanes_flag_reaches_run_gate` and `test_dirty_canonical_checkout_refuses_gate_before_any_lane` now give their candidate a copy of the running `tools/`, because a candidate without the gate code would be re-executed; their assertions are unchanged. Focused MacBook run: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_candidate_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 47` exited 0: `Ran 47 tests in 60.488s`, `OK`. Audits: `audit_docs.py` `Documentation audit passed.`; `audit_test_quality.py` `hard issues: 0`; `audit_item_reachability.py` `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`, exit 0; `git diff --check` clean. Papercut line `2026-09-23T22:44-07:00` moved verbatim to `papercuts-fixed-2026-09-23.md`; `papercuts.md` 83 → 82 lines.

## 2026-09-26 — Healthy waiting gates keep both lock records fresh

Failure cases listed before implementation: a competing claim kills a healthy waiting gate; the gate marker stops refreshing while the landing lock refreshes; genuine stale-lock recovery stops working; either held record survives gate exit.
Finding verified against `d0904603ffd7b9fd0070cae97deba8d4506837a2`: the outer gate refreshed only its marker, while its landing lock could cross the unchanged 1300-second stale limit during the unchanged 3600-second mirror wait. `run_gate` now sends one heartbeat that refreshes both held records; recovery, timeout limits and validators are unchanged.
Regression first: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_waiting_gate_survives_competing_claim_after_1301_seconds --expected-tests 1` exited 1: `AssertionError: Healthy waiting gate was killed as stale`, `Ran 1 test in 0.261s`, `FAILED (failures=1)` (`regression-before.log`). ## 2026-09-25 — The root surface digest no longer depends on the checkout folder name

`tools/module_surface.py` loaded the root `__init__.py` under `ROOT.name`, the checkout's folder leaf. That name enters the dump through `MODULE_SURFACE module=…` and every module-typed value, and `_stable_string` rewrites only absolute path prefixes, so `ROOT_QT_DIGEST` in `tests/test_module_surface.py` matched only in a folder called `ankiscape_fork` (the runner mirror and the canonical checkout). The root initializer now loads under the fixed `ROOT_PACKAGE_NAME = "ankiscape_fork"`, the name the pin was recorded under, so the pin stays valid without a re-pin. `ankiscape` was rejected: `pkg_alias.py` binds it to an alias that deliberately skips the root initializer, and loading the root under it would replace that alias.

Proof (local Mac Studio, `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen .venv-qt/bin/python tools/module_surface.py --digest-only __init__.py`, Python 3.13.5): before the fix, the worktree folder `goal-surface-digest-folder-2026-09-25` gave `ba36cff2a9afbf6e5efa1255257d02b1ee162482a5ced46974f29c398ee97f22` and a `git archive HEAD` copy in a folder named `ankiscape_fork` gave `74044844e6abb546bc2d895f337ec92c0afe66ddc9002931d65dc12256cd5f4e`; after the fix, both the worktree folder and a patched copy in a folder named `renamed_checkout` give `74044844e6abb546bc2d895f337ec92c0afe66ddc9002931d65dc12256cd5f4e`, equal to `ROOT_QT_DIGEST`.

Focused MacBook proof: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt` with the eight `ModuleSurfaceTest` methods selected by name, `--expected-tests 8`, exited 0: `Ran 8 tests in 20.410s`, `OK` (log SHA-256 `4371bea95c4ebc644affc45d428930a71a6719e5cbf4b0ef4e2cb46d171f2c1c`). The ninth method, `test_root_initializer_is_reported_as_unavailable_without_aqt`, skips on every remote lane because the plain interpreter has no `aqt`, so `--select test_module_surface. --expected-tests 9` exits 1 with `error: remote focused lane false pass: tests=9, skipped=1; expected exactly 9 tests and no skips`. Memory Bank readers: `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` on the plain lane exited 0: `Ran 46 tests in 60.520s`, `OK` (log SHA-256 `8728ffc307b8dbf8420ba12f7378fba1a0bc2179510b371847cae71433f92e07`, run on the final Memory Bank text). `python3 tools/audit_docs.py` → `Audited 871 Markdown and 452 HTML files.` / `Documentation audit passed.`; `python3 tools/audit_test_quality.py` → `4879 tests across 284 files` / `hard issues: 0` / `structural clone candidates: 31 groups`; `python3 tools/audit_item_reachability.py` exits 0 with `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`; `git diff --check` is clean; the slop gate on a temporary index exited 0 with empty output. Raw logs sit in `ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-surface-digest-folder-2026-09-25/` under the git common dir.

## 2026-09-25 — Generators prove their own output is unchanged

Four refactor slices proved generator output identity with hand-built hash pipelines: Smithing and Summoning with `shasum -a 256` over three named files, and three diary slices with a 13-file `find data -type f | sort | xargs shasum -a 256` manifest (`06434752e309f79186855049947882711b39914321324de89a180e994614825d`), which gives `70432108e5c1a8ba465f70a70b1b680b2839d764d46ce55b1d1f5220d5cd39ce` for the same files when run as `find .` from the scratch root. `tools/generation/outputs.py` `write_or_check` now owns output writing for `tools/generate_smithing_data.py`, `tools/generate_summoning_catalog.py` and `tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py`. Each takes `--check`: it regenerates in memory, compares bytes with every file a write would touch (Smithing and Summoning include `generated/source_digests.json`, now rendered by `source_digest_manifest`, which replaces `save_source_digests`), names each differing file, prints `OUTPUT_CHECK: <n> of <m> files unchanged`, and exits 1 on any difference. Without `--check`, a generator writes only the files whose bytes changed. No generated file changed.

Proof on this tree: `python3 tools/generate_smithing_data.py --check` and `python3 tools/generate_summoning_catalog.py --check` each exit 0 with `OUTPUT_CHECK: 2 of 2 files unchanged`; `python3 tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py memory-bank/source-audits/achievement-diary-harvest-2026-07-26.json --items-yml /Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Downloads/game-main/data/cfg/items.yml --check` exits 0 with `OUTPUT_CHECK: 13 of 13 files unchanged`. Against temporary copies, one trailing space in `smithing_data.py` gives exit 1 with `OUTPUT_CHECK: 1 of 2 files unchanged`; a Summoning manifest missing its entry gives exit 1 naming `source_digests.json`; a trailing space in `varrock.py` plus a deleted `seers.py` gives exit 1 naming both and `OUTPUT_CHECK: 11 of 13 files unchanged`. A diary write into an empty directory wrote 13 files that then check `13 of 13` unchanged, and write runs of all three generators on this tree wrote nothing (`git status --short` lists no data file).

Focused MacBook proof: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_generated_skill_data. --select test_catalog_scan. --select test_condition_keys. --expected-tests 7` exited 0: `Ran 7 tests in 2.171s`, `OK`. With the byte comparison mutated to a text comparison, `--select test_generated_skill_data. --expected-tests 5` exited 1: `FAILED (failures=1)` in `test_output_check_names_every_stale_file_without_writing_until_asked`, which pins that a CRLF-only difference and a missing file are both reported and that a check writes nothing. Raw logs: `ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-generator-output-proof-2026-09-25/` in the git common dir.

Checks: `python3 tools/audit_docs.py` → `Audited 871 Markdown and 452 HTML files.` / `Documentation audit passed.`; `python3 tools/audit_test_quality.py` → `4880 tests across 284 files` / `hard issues: 0` / `structural clone candidates: 31 groups`; `python3 tools/audit_item_reachability.py` exit 0, `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`; `git diff --check` clean; slop gate against a temporary index exit 0. After the Memory Bank edits, `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` on the MacBook plain lane exited 0: `Ran 46 tests in 61.063s`, `OK`.

## 2026-09-26 — Generator writes match the check byte for byte

Review of `6f908d21` found that `write_or_check` compared UTF-8 bytes but wrote with `Path.write_text`, which translates `\n` to the platform line ending; on Windows a fresh write would fail its own `--check` and every write run would rewrite the same files. The writer now writes `text.encode("utf-8")` with `write_bytes`, the bytes the check compares, and the test's unchanged fixture and final assertion are byte-explicit. Proof: the three generator `--check` runs from the section above each exit 0 with `2 of 2`, `2 of 2` and `13 of 13` files unchanged, write runs of all three leave `git status --short` without a data file, and `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_generated_skill_data. --expected-tests 5` exited 0: `Ran 5 tests in 0.229s`, `OK`.

## 2026-09-25 — Tests get one name each, and short selectors match whole names

Branch `goal/test-names-selectors-2026-09-25` from main `d0904603`. Closes papercuts 48, 50, 60, 64 and, from the same triage class, 72 and 75 (moved verbatim to `papercuts-fixed-2026-09-23.md`).

`run_tests.py` now discovers with `top_level_dir` at the checkout, so every ID starts with `tests.` and a module that other tests import is no longer loaded under a second name. It exits 2 and names every ID discovered more than once. A selector first matches whole dotted segments (`test_achievement_diaries`, `tests.test_x`, `promotion.test_x`) and falls back to a case-insensitive fragment only when no whole segment matches. `TargetLevelOrderWidgetTest` now composes the main-menu fixture, as `tests/ui_flows/` already does, instead of subclassing `MainMenuWidgetTest` and inheriting its 421 tests; two bare `from test_x import` lines became `from tests.test_x import`. The remote full Qt lane runs `run_tests.py`, not `-m unittest discover tests`, so it gets the same names and the duplicate check; `README.md` and `README-tests.md` name the same command.

Design choice: the triage proposed a `MainMenuWidgetFixture` base class carved out of `tests/test_main_menu_widget.py` (19,883 lines), paid for by deleting blank lines so the slop gate's growth rail would pass. The gate forbids paying a line budget with stripped spacing, and the composition pattern already exists in `tests/ui_flows/`, so the large file is unchanged.

After: 4916 IDs in both lanes, all starting `tests.`, 0 duplicates; plain and Qt lists identical.

## 2026-09-25 — Cleanup-required quest actions without an emitter are pinned at the eight known ones

A stage cleanup in `data/quest/stage_cleanups.py` can add a required action as free text, and the runtime stage gate (`logic_pure/logic_pure_quest.py`, `quest_action_proof_completed_pure`) accepts it only once that exact string is in `quest_action_proofs`. Only two writers put strings there: a stage's `auto_actions` (`quest_action_proofs_after_stage_actions_pure`) and the emitted proofs of `QUEST_ACTION_PROOF_RULES`. Nothing checked the whole cleanup catalog against them (papercut line 8, the Within the Light case). `TestQuestActionProofWriterOwnership.test_every_cleanup_required_action_has_a_runtime_emitter` in `tests/test_quest_action_proofs.py` now walks every `QUEST_STAGE_REQUIREMENT_CLEANUPS_BY_ID` entry, collects each `add_required_actions` string found in neither set as a `(quest id, stage, action)` triple, and asserts that set equals `_UNEMITTED_CLEANUP_ACTIONS`. It covers only cleanup-added required actions, not every quest action. It lives there, not in `tests/test_item_reachability_audit.py` (1899 lines, which the slop gate refuses to grow), and reuses that module's `_quest_action_owners`.

The catalog has 11 such strings; 3 have an emitter (all proof-rule outputs) and 8 do not. The test accepts exactly those 8 as a pinned baseline, so a new unemitted cleanup action fails, and so does a pinned triple that gains an emitter or disappears until its fix edits the set. The 8 stay unresolved and papercut line 8 stays open for them. They are player-visible defects, recorded as `source_blocked` in the goal-priority matrix row "Eight quest stages that can never be finished" and in `papercuts.md`: `mournings_end_part_i` stage 3 `Have Oronwen repair the mourner trousers`; `cold_war` stage 2 `Build the clockwork penguin on a crafting table 3`; `the_fremennik_isles` stage 5 `Make a Fremennik round shield`; `spirits_of_the_elid` stage 2 `Cast Telegrab for the water channel puzzle`; `enakhras_lament` stage 5 `Cast Crumble Undead during the boneguard fight`; `in_aid_of_the_myreque` stage 6 `Enchant sapphire for the Myreque preparations`; `the_eyes_of_glouphrie` stage 3 `Collect a bucket of sap from an evergreen tree`; `legacy_of_seergaze` stage 5 `Cast Enchant Level 2 Jewellery for the emerald preparations`. No data changed and no expected-failure marker was added.

Proof, raw logs in `ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-quest-action-emitter-check-2026-09-25/` under the git common dir:

## 2026-09-25 — A sync can silently undo another desktop's game progress (reproduced, not fixed)

Confirmed the defect found by the review-log catch-up spec (`memory-bank/specs/review-log-catch-up.md` section 11, commit `220527e0`). The game reads its save from collection config once, when the profile opens (`__init__.py:457-460`, `profileLoaded`), and every later game write puts that memory copy back (`__init__.py:452-454`, `core/storage.py:51-54`). The add-on registers no sync or collection hook. In Anki 25.09.4 (`d52ca669`, both lanes) the profile-open hook runs before the automatic sync (`aqt/main.py:568-569`); a full download reopens the same `Collection` over the downloaded file (`aqt/sync.py:238-250`) and fires `sync_did_finish` (`aqt/main.py:1105-1108`). By source reading at tag `25.09.4`, a normal sync also replaces all config from the side that changed last (`rslib/src/sync/collection/changes.rs:131-132, 214-240`, `rslib/src/storage/config/mod.rs:79-95`); that path has no test.

Reproduction (Qt lane only; plain has no Anki): the test first asserted the downloaded save and `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt --select test_full_sync_download_keeps_the_downloaded_save_on_the_next_game_write --expected-tests 1` → `Ran 1 test in 0.297s`, `FAILED (failures=1)`, `AssertionError: 120.0 != 4500.0 : the pre-download save in memory overwrote the downloaded save`, exit 1 (log SHA-256 `1afcc466d83969637453f852d003b716019fbe3162c10b750cc88988482f6b81`). Module run `--select test_storage_runtime --expected-tests 5` → `Ran 5 tests in 1.137s`, `FAILED (failures=1)`, the same single failure (log SHA-256 `31bdbe0e5f965e6156f5fa0d36c81d33a6e486d96763274f41f85023184ac349`).

A committed failing test would fail every Qt gate, because `run_remote_tests.py` accepts only an `OK` summary, so the cross-lineage review of `095dcacb` had the test pin the defect instead: `tests/test_storage_runtime.py::StorageRuntimeTest::test_next_game_write_after_full_sync_download_puts_back_the_old_save` keeps every setup and download assertion and expects the old 120.0 in the final one, whose message says to flip it to 4500.0 when the fix lands. `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt --select test_storage_runtime. --expected-tests 5` → `Ran 5 tests in 1.251s`, `OK`, exit 0 (log `review-fix-qt-module.txt`, SHA-256 `d8d75a95781f5ce0836fc390b13cc1f6f0652f86824b9a7b3831b8f06e0c5f4a`). Neighbours: Qt `--select test_cape_tradeability. --expected-tests 4` → `Ran 4 tests in 0.952s`, `OK` (SHA-256 `e6131a7cad2dead7e999ff12f82c53dcfc65ca8d9cba496e4317ec754433c09b`); plain `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --select test_miniquest_ledger. --expected-tests 65` → `Ran 65 tests in 59.916s`, `OK`, exit 0 (SHA-256 `59a425acbd4eb97ba5b4a6f906945346a0ee24f23c852f1a3ffac57b988e6dd4`). `audit_docs.py` passed (871 Markdown, 452 HTML files), `audit_test_quality.py` reports hard issues: 0, `audit_item_reachability.py` exits 0 with first line `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`, `worktree_guard.py audit` reports the layout safe, and `git diff --check` is clean.

Result: a sync can still wipe progress made on another desktop; the defect is reproduced and pinned, and the fix waits on the owner's choice among three options (recommended: reload after sync) in [design-thread queue §3.39](design-thread-queue.md); the matrix carries the row as `owner_gated`.

## 2026-09-25 — Papercut log cut to its open entries

A per-line triage of main's `memory-bank/papercuts.md` at `d0904603` (81 entries) gave each line a disposition with evidence checked against main, and a grouping pass merged the lines into cause classes and ranked fix slices (`ankiscape_worktrees/.orchestrator/papercut-triage-2026-09-25/verdicts.json`, SHA-256 `3ad224dea2475649a64e34c6e917e419d87cba762010185cfc5d9666e648a7c0`). Dispositions: `fixed-before` 12, `operator-error` 31, `external-blocker` 12, `design-decision` 8, `open-defect` 16, `paused-feature` 2. The 63 addressed lines moved verbatim into [papercuts-fixed-2026-09-25.md](papercuts-fixed-2026-09-25.md), one section per merged class with its disposition and evidence; the 16 open-defect and 2 paused-feature lines stay in `papercuts.md`.

Open classes, each with the grouping's ranked fix slice:
- Test discovery gives tests the wrong names (lines 48, 50, 64): slice 1, discover with `top_level_dir=root`, fail on a duplicate test ID, and turn the main-menu widget setup into a fixture base.
- A short selector also picks up sibling modules (line 60): slice 1, match a selector against whole dotted segments first.
- An orphaned remote lane makes the next run fail at once (lines 51, 63, 67): slice 2, wait for the pending report up to `QT_LANE_TIMEOUT` and fail only when the cap runs out.
- The old gate code judges a candidate that changes the gate (line 79): slice 3, re-run the candidate's own gate when its gate code differs.
- Papercut lines have no fixed format and moves are done by hand (line 46): slice 4, one line format, a move tool with a sorted no-loss check, and an audit_docs check.
- A quest stage requires an action that nothing ever produces (line 8): slice 5, a catalog-wide test for cleanup action strings, pinning the 8 known unemitted ones.
- The folder name of the checkout leaks into the surface digest (line 53): slice 6, a fixed package name in `tools/module_surface.py`.
- The remote run copies the live working tree after the lock wait (line 73): slice 7, fingerprint the tree before and after the lock and stop if it changed.
- Generator output has no fixed way to prove it is unchanged (line 69): slice 8, a `--check` mode in `tools/generate_achievement_diary_data.py`.
- Two rulesync rules both claim root (line 39): slice 9, `root: false` on the native-verification rule and an audit_docs check.
- `stages` means a count on quests but a list elsewhere (line 21): slice 10, rename `QuestDefinition.stages` to `stage_count`.
- Remote focused runs are only checked on the remote host, after the lock wait (line 43): no ranked slice; the grouping names none.

Proof (documentation-only; branch `goal/papercut-triage-record-2026-09-25`): a sorted comparison of `git show d0904603:memory-bank/papercuts.md` entries against the remaining open entries plus the moved lines prints `base=81 open_after=18 new_papercut=1 moved=63 sorted_equal=True` and `overlap_open_vs_all_fixed=0` against every `papercuts-fixed-*.md` file (both sorted sets SHA-256 `5ffbd1d311b0a1e94c92cac2b491477b822b6a1aeee7f85ecd6b0e90fb3cd3a3`). `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_promotion_gate --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet --expected-tests 46` exited 0: `Ran 46 tests in 59.805s`, `OK` (log SHA-256 `33cb43533ca05c67e0b70fa2683048885557f300b9d5899cfc0ea80127098a9e`). `audit_docs.py`: `Audited 872 Markdown and 452 HTML files.` / `Documentation audit passed.`; `audit_test_quality.py`: `4879 tests across 284 files` / `hard issues: 0`; `audit_item_reachability.py` exits 0 with first line `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`, equal to the baseline; `worktree_guard.py audit` reports the layout safe; `git diff --check` is clean. The throwaway build and proof scripts and raw logs sit in `ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-papercut-triage-record-2026-09-25/` under the git common dir.

Review fixes (same branch, follow-up commit): the native-acceptance disposition no longer cites the untracked goal-blocker file, which this branch does not carry, and now states that the owner has not yet chosen among the three routes in `design-thread-queue.md` §3.38. Two evidence citations now point at their supporting statements: the papercut no-loss proof at `progress.md:977`, and `ITEM_DEFINITIONS` as the `Tuple[ItemDefinition, ...]` from `apply_item_asset_overrides` (`core/constants.py:499`, `core/item_registry.py:317`). Proof: the same focused remote plain run (`--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46`) exited 0 with `Ran 46 tests` / `OK` (log `remote-plain-review-fix.log` in the evidence directory); the four audits and `git diff --check` report as in the proof above.

## 2026-09-23 — Processing-pack refill admission leaves the carried overlay's main function

Branch `goal/carried-pack-refill-2026-09-23`, from main `2021da75`. The processing-pack refill admission (merge the recipe, carried-potion and, for advanced layouts only, presence requirements; then force a bank run when the pack is short and the bank can refill it) moved from `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` into `_processing_refill_due` in the same module. The caller keeps `bank_run_requested or (is_processing and ...)`, so the short-circuit order, the RNG draws, undo snapshots and saved data are unchanged. No signature, caller, public surface or UI changed.

Measured with lizard 1.24.0, complexipy 7.0.1 and radon 6.0.1 (`lizard --csv`, `complexipy <file> --output-format json -q -i`, `radon hal -f -j`, as in the slop gate): `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` went from CCN 157, cognitive 208, Halstead difficulty 10.76 to CCN 152, cognitive 205, Halstead difficulty 10.83. The new helper measures CCN 6, cognitive 5, Halstead difficulty 1.0. The main function still fails CCN and cognitive; this slice only removes one responsibility from it.

Coverage probes, each restored byte-identically (SHA-256 `9050eab38cf20b454f14e5b6eca544c9e1ef4ff2970aeac72677c8f088808440`): with the carried-potion merge deleted (P1), or the presence merge applied outside advanced layouts (P2), 327 selected carried tests all passed (`OK (skipped=30)`). P2 is caught outside that selection by `test_cannonball_review_reward_and_mould_roll_back_on_undo` (`{'Steel bar': 1, 'Ammo mould': 1} != {'Steel bar': 0, 'Ammo mould': 1, 'Cannonball': 4}`). P1 had no catching test, so `test_pack_without_its_potion_restocks_it_from_the_bank_before_crafting` now drives the real kernel and fails under P1 with `AssertionError: False is not true`.

Proof: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_carried_processing_reconciliation. --select TestProcessingPackOverlay --select test_crafting_advanced_layout_refills_consumed_thread_from_bank_before_resuming --select test_crafting_carried_overlay --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --select test_skilling_boost_logic. --select test_cannonball_review_reward_and_mould_roll_back_on_undo --expected-tests 92` gave `Ran 92 tests in 17.032s` and `OK`. `audit_docs.py`, `audit_test_quality.py` (4869 tests across 282 files, hard issues 0), `worktree_guard.py audit` and `git diff --check` passed. `audit_item_reachability.py` exited 1 with the unchanged baseline `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`. The full lanes were not run here; the promotion gate runs them on the merged tree.

Raw evidence is in the common Git directory under `ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-carried-pack-refill-2026-09-23/`. SHA-256: before-lizard.csv `e20abc164ee2005880c112b43d31ff95e8e68a3df9bfd827ddf5550f6f1aae14`, before-complexipy.json `2953a4e296ffb3b772a4dcedb05b13afc26ad73346ebc88ff496f6287d95c4c0`, before-radon.json `e1e45ca54c89885598d075f95f26c6c93c2caca515d0b41ddfe4879b7d73a6ef`, after-lizard.csv `07243d8cf73041d55bd31532dff62584b500eaa14727d577dedbfe7bce82c882`, after-complexipy.json `b907291f644f4912165f01ed82e4a2f2c827d6974fda2e2933716cbf6fc6b866`, after-radon.json `1da316b7f2c2b78f07bef5af0095382ec572eb6f8e5be0aec0dede04f5fd9b1c`, probe-P1.log `2e8f5a35f31b7ddd390f885b33e670b23c152950ed746394b38302390d538d23`, probe-P2.log `8b534ab970547af63d9997617ec894a96608c3f6618d40c2c14689fff55f16fb`, probe2-P1.log `753d9addd7c469ebbf40c9bf94d6a40dd43ee2d872ef20b76f5a44c9069d66ac`, probe2-P2.log `ea844b4cbf094bd5201d01da7fd420d72c9e76073138c24774b5cc4ae662a434`, probe-survivors-summary.txt `f97eb36e89e5c484abf778f5f93acf9ca71747fd7ac706b4947718f2f4f2e2e9`, probe2-and-final-summary.txt `8290f3380834f8c5320711b3f4ae23552d93305fbdc194f5a750299395d85eb3`, remote-plain-focused.log (90-test run before the new test) `327dcdee41369424789054187771233870ccba8b99cf3f30fdd3d882d402742f`, remote-plain-focused-final.log `37aafc18f1a14c5b818200d0e81a400fe061f8d1da64bed4ec0f603d4fcf9e71`, reachability.txt `7a1529f374f1acac2b700838ca08055939a39053dd4d4b57f7a70aa082928c19`.

## 2026-09-23 — Combat preview risk classification has its own module

The combat target preview kept the enemy's threat, the risk band, poison, Wilderness, hazard and encounter state inside one function beside the player's attack projection. That half now lives in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_risk.py` as four functions: incoming damage after weakening, protective and dragonfire gear; Wilderness risk; environmental hazard; and the risk band plus final preview assembly. `combat_target_preview_pure` keeps the lookup gate and the melee, ranged and Magic attack-roll and max-hit branches unchanged, and passes its attack fields to the risk half. The engine drops from 2906 to 2755 lines; the new module has 218. Previews stay value-identical and keep their key order. Pure reads such as finisher, hazard and weakening conversions now run in a different order, which could only change which error surfaces when two independent malformed inputs coexist. The preserved Magic candidate `a8137bab` and the Dream transaction were not touched.

Measured with lizard 1.24.0, complexipy 7.0.1 and radon 6.0.1 (CCN / cognitive / Halstead difficulty). Before: `combat_target_preview_pure` 48 / 50 / 7.40. After: `combat_target_preview_pure` 20 / 21 / 4.31; `_combat_preview_incoming_damage` 11 / 8 / 2.57; `_combat_preview_wilderness_risk` 4 / 3 / 1.00; `_combat_preview_environmental_hazard` 3 / 2 / 1.00; `_combat_preview_with_risk` 13 / 15 / 3.47. The target remains above the limits of 15 because its remainder is the lookup gate plus the if/elif chains that hold the reserved Magic branch and its spell and rune display; closing it needs the owner's Magic decision. The installed slop gate, run on the staged pair and then unstaged, exited 0 with no output.

Proof. ## 2026-09-23 — Quest catalog loader under the complexity gate

The quest catalog reader in `data/quest/catalog.py` now turns one catalog record into a quest through separate readers for required quests, required skills, XP rewards, item rewards (catalog items, replacement, then additions), and lamp rewards, which share one filter for well-formed keyed rows. Item-pair parsing splits into the list reader, one list entry, and one mapping entry. Each replacement table is still applied after its catalog value is built, in the original order. No public name, quest data, quest content or user interface changed; no source was harvested and no file moved.

Measured with lizard 1.24.0 / complexipy 7.0.1 / radon 6.0.1 (limits CCN 15, cognitive 15, Halstead difficulty 80). `_quest_from_catalog`: before CCN 35, cognitive 35, Halstead 2.1176; after CCN 5, cognitive 2, Halstead 0.5. `_catalog_item_pairs`: before CCN 15, cognitive 33, Halstead 2.6923; after CCN 6, cognitive 8, Halstead 0. New helpers peak at CCN 6, cognitive 6, Halstead 1.5; complexipy reports no FAILED function in the file. The slop gate run against a temporary index holding the change exited 0 with empty output.

Data identity: from the worktree root, `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 -c "import pkg_alias; from ankiscape.data.quest import ledger, catalog; ..."` printed `repr` of `_quest_from_catalog(row)` for all 194 catalog rows, then every one of the 198 loaded `QUESTS` in order. Before and after SHA-256 are both `78d57db4f78f291c7a9cc5665c9d90b269bfb477fb410b0d671a71e09adaf940` (3724284 bytes, `cmp` identical). A throwaway differential of the original item-pair parser against the new one over 3157 shaped and malformed inputs found 0 mismatches.

Remote proof, from the worktree: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain` with one `--select <module>.` each for test_cabin_fever_completion, test_capes_and_distinctions, test_combat_quest_gate, test_conquest, test_item_reachability_audit, test_lost_tribe_completion, test_logic, test_miniquest_ledger, test_music_log, test_quest_action_proofs, test_pre_eoc_quest_catalog_builder, test_quest_catalog, test_rum_deal_completion, test_shop_logic, test_runtime_actions, test_undo_seam_pestcontrol, test_zogre_flesh_eaters_completion and test_direct_action_undo_seam, `--expected-tests 774`, exited 0: `Ran 774 tests in 275.908s` / `OK`. The two quest-importing modules that need Qt ran on the qt lane: `--select test_main_menu_widget.` plus the eight `ModuleSurfaceTest` methods other than `test_root_initializer_is_reported_as_unavailable_without_aqt`, `--expected-tests 850`, exited 0: `Ran 850 tests in 258.907s` / `OK`. That excluded test skips in both lanes by design, because it runs the plain interpreter, and the focused runner rejects any skip.

## 2026-09-23 — Shop access locks and shop-state sanitizing split into single-rule helpers

Behaviour-preserving cleanup from the "Remaining exposed-function cleanup" row, shop logic family. `shop_access_lock_reason_pure` now reads the rule and label, then dispatches on `kind` through `_ACCESS_LOCK_REASONS` to one helper per gate (`_quest_access_lock_reason`, `_quest_points_access_lock_reason`, `_skill_access_lock_reason`, `_skill_total_access_lock_reason`); unknown kinds, including `wilderness`, still return `None`. `sanitize_shop_state_pure` now assembles the save from `_sanitize_market_clock`, `_sanitize_stock_state` / `_sanitize_stock_rows` / `_sanitize_stock_row`, `_sanitize_shop_events` and `_sanitize_transaction_counts`. The quest gate's two integer parses reuse `_clean_int(value, 0)`, which is the same `int()` with a `(TypeError, ValueError)` fallback to 0. `logic/shop_logic.py` re-exports the ten new helpers in the `name as name` form, because `tests/shops/test_module_boundaries.py` requires the aggregator to publish every function `logic/shop/state.py` defines; its surface digest stays `53a900c9b4641264f4b3cfdb1a613b2672316b569eff35c45c5202aaf7c0e2f0`. No UI file changed.

Measured with lizard 1.24.0, complexipy 7.0.1 and radon 6.0.1 (CCN / cognitive / Halstead difficulty). Before: `sanitize_shop_state_pure` 21 / 42 / 3.65, `shop_access_lock_reason_pure` 29 / 42 / 3.97. After: `sanitize_shop_state_pure` 2 / 1 / 0.5, `shop_access_lock_reason_pure` 3 / 2 / 1.0; new helpers `_quest_access_lock_reason` 12 / 13 / 2.81, `_sanitize_stock_state` 6 / 7 / 1.0, `_sanitize_stock_rows` 6 / 7 / 1.5, `_sanitize_transaction_counts` 5 / 5 / 2.0, `_skill_total_access_lock_reason` 5 / 4 / 1.0, `_sanitize_shop_events` 4 / 3 / 1.0, `_quest_points_access_lock_reason` and `_skill_access_lock_reason` 4 / 3 / 1.2, `_sanitize_stock_row` 3 / 2 / 2.33, `_sanitize_market_clock` 2 / 1 / 0. The module's remaining cognitive violation is the untouched `_item_tradeable` at 18.

Equivalence: an in-process differential run loaded `git show HEAD:logic/shop/state.py` beside the new module and printed `access comparisons 113160 mismatches 0 distinct outcomes 60` (357 real shop and service rows plus 2,403 synthetic rules, 41 players) and `sanitize comparisons 3002 mismatches 0` (JSON-identical output, input unmutated). One unreachable divergence remains: an unhashable `access.kind` (for example a list) now raises `TypeError` from the dict lookup instead of returning `None`; every shipped kind is a string.
