## 2026-09-24 — Carried requirements: three exposed functions under the complexity gate

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", carried family. `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_requirements.py` keeps the names, signatures and callers of `_processing_requirements_for`, `_agility_casual_layout_for` and `_agility_carried_requirement_gaps`; `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_overlay.py` is untouched. Processing requirements now delegate each skill's own rule to one helper: summoning's resolver, Fletching's carried-then-bank material choice, the Coal bag deduction, the owned gilded altar's marrentill, and the first stocked Runecrafting essence. The two Agility functions share one helper for the requirements nothing worn already covers, and the casual layout takes its auto-eat food from a second helper. Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty), before on `a45c92d8` (file identical to `2021da75`) then after: `_processing_requirements_for` 26/42/5.52 → 12/12/3.27; `_agility_casual_layout_for` 24/33/4.50 → 12/13/2.80; `_agility_carried_requirement_gaps` 16/16/3.69 → 9/6/3.44. Highest new helper: `_agility_unequipped_requirements` CCN 10, cognitive 9. The slop gate (`slop_gate.py` against a temporary copy of the index with the file staged) exited 0 with no output.

## 2026-09-23 — Native verify refuses while Anki runs (papercut triage)

`tools/native/session_guard.py verify` measured the owner's collection and could print `"unchanged": true` while an Anki process was still open and able to write it; on 2026-09-21 `guard-final-verify.stdout` did exactly that before the later post-quit verify reported `unchanged: false`. `verify` now calls the existing fail-closed `require_anki_stopped()` after reading the receipt and before measuring, so a running Anki (or a failed `pgrep` probe) exits 2 with no JSON result. The shared refusal message and every other guard behaviour are unchanged.

## 2026-09-23 — Orchestrator run: landing order, MacBook contention and native checks

Step 2 landed as `4f345789` before steps 5 and 6. The owner order 4, 1, 5, 6, 2, 3 sets priority; step 2 has no dependency on 5 or 6, and it was the first slice ready for the serial gate. Receipt: 8 checks, plain 5728 tests 626.911s skipped=1366, Qt 5728 tests 1170.895s skipped=5. Remote focused runs from several authors waited behind the gate's mirror lock (~30 min per gate); that contention is expected.

## 2026-09-24 — Promotion gate lanes no longer pay for the mirror queue

Gate `1fc26752` failed on 2026-09-23 with `TIMEOUT: exceeded 1000 seconds` and `TIMEOUT: exceeded 1300 seconds` because each remote lane's fixed limit also counted the runner's wait for `~/Library/Caches/ankiscape-remote-test-host.lock` behind focused runs.

Design choice: the gate itself takes the mirror lock before its remote lanes and holds it across both; each lane runner inherits the locked descriptor (`ANKISCAPE_INHERITED_MIRROR_LOCK_FD` plus `pass_fds`) and uses it instead of queueing. The lane limits (plain 1,000 s, Qt 1,300 s), the outer wall-clock guard and its `TIMEOUT: exceeded N seconds` message are untouched, so they now bound only preflight, sync and execution, exactly as before for a lane's own work. The rejected alternative, having the runner start the limit at lock acquisition, would widen the gate's outer guard by the wait and leave a hung rsync unbounded until the wider guard fired. The gate's wait is bounded separately by `REMOTE_MIRROR_LOCK_TIMEOUT = 3600.0` (longer than another gate's whole 2,300 s lane pair), refreshes the gate heartbeat every second while it waits, logs `held after N seconds` or `TIMEOUT: waited 3600 seconds for <path>`, and on timeout fails both lanes as `(mirror lock wait timed out)` without starting them. The runner accepts an inherited descriptor only if it names the lock file and a nonblocking flock on it succeeds, so the variable cannot jump the queue. Pending-run reconciliation, serial ownership through the one lock, and the floors are unchanged.

Proof, evidence in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-gate-lock-wait-2026-09-24/`:
- `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_remote_test_host --select test_promotion_gate --select test_gate_lock_claim --select test_remote_mirror_lock --expected-tests 62` exited 0 with `Ran 62 tests in 71.457s` / `OK` (`remote-plain-focused-4.log`, sha256 `c3b7f82fa6d049de948990b8d7a42142dd9d2496298ab1cd0873833556eca6ca`). The first commit attempt was refused by slop-gate (`_run_gate_checks 30 (was 15)`, `tests/test_promotion_gate.py: 1026 lines (was 935)`; `commit-hook.log`, `a8a305e8063b8fe83c0ee3fab8b05c77e265b9754c90a4b8e6fa14b0856ac8db`), so the check loop became `_run_check_sequence` and the timing tests moved to `tests/promotion/test_remote_mirror_lock.py`; the pre-move pass is `remote-plain-focused-3.log` (`752b5b8310ceff8593e8df2ecf240515bc19f9e40d4a510932058ce6bf72e820`).

## 2026-09-24 — Qt lane speed: same tests, about a third less time

The offscreen Qt lane was slow for three test-side reasons, found by per-test timing on the MacBook. (1) Main-menu tests left their windows alive: tests clear `_captured`, and panels detached with `setParent(None)` outlive their dialog, so about 47,000 widgets stayed live after one pass of `test_main_menu_widget`. Every later app-wide `setStyle("Fusion")` or palette change repolished them (10 to 15 s per `setUpClass` in the Temple Trekking, Mobilising Armies and Gnome Restaurant panels, and 9 to 29 s per dark-mode main-menu test). `MainMenuWidgetTest.tearDown` now deletes every top-level window the test created, parented or not; a probe then found 0 leaked widgets after the module. (2) 23 unpatched `build_reachability_report()` calls across six modules each rebuilt the same live report (about 8.6 s each). `tests/reachability/live_report.py` builds it once per process and hands each caller a deep copy (2 ms); every test that patches game data still calls `build_reachability_report()` itself. (3) `setUp` built a default Mining dialog that 380 of the 421 main-menu tests never use; `dialog` is now a `cached_property` that builds it on first use. No product code, test, assertion, skip, limit or floor changed.

Discovered test ids are identical before and after (`run_tests.py --list` on both lanes: 5730 at a45c92d8 and on this tree, `cmp` of the sorted id lists exit 0); the brief's 5,731 does not match a45c92d8. Full Qt lane through `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py <tree> qt`: unchanged a45c92d8 export `/tmp/qtspeed-head`, `Ran 5730 tests in 1160.597s`, `OK (skipped=5)` (load 36.91 at 00:22, 9.53 at 00:48); this tree, `Ran 5730 tests in 764.065s`, `OK (skipped=5)` (load 11.53 at 00:04, 12.76 at 00:21).

Per-module seconds, out-of-tree harness over the whole suite in one process (baseline 1286.4 s total, after 788.5 s): `test_item_reachability_audit` 247.5 → 100.2; `test_main_menu_widget` 183.8 → 82.2; `tests.test_main_menu_widget` 114.2 → 76.4; `skills.test_target_level_order` 142.3 → 75.3; `test_early_quest_item_routes` 8.9 → 0.2; `test_skeletal_wyvern` 8.9 → 0.3; `test_potion_flask_acquisition` 8.5 → 0.0; `test_big_chompy_completion` 8.8 → 0.4; `test_achievement_diaries` 8.8 → 8.6 (now pays the one shared build); `test_temple_trekking` 15.9 → 0.6; `test_mobilising_armies` 14.2 → 0.3; `test_gnome_restaurant` 22.3 → 12.1. The harness run fails `test_module_surface...test_root_initializer_digest_under_qt` in its scratch directory before and after; the runner lanes pass it.

## 2026-09-24 — Root cleanup: review answer, reviewer-context choices and skill-availability fingerprint

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", root family. The skill-availability fingerprint and the catalog scan behind it are pure reads of the save and the skill catalogs, so they moved to `core/skill_availability.py`; the root imports `skill_availability_fingerprint` under its old private name `_skill_availability_fingerprint` and passes `_state.player_data`. The module-level loops that built the fingerprint's key lists became small functions there, which also removes nine leaked loop temporaries from the root namespace. `resolve_review_context_action` hands the Shooting Star notice to `_resolve_star_notice_choice` and the Pit reward or Phoenix egg roll to `_review_choice_outcome`; its snapshot, undo entry and commit order are unchanged. `on_good_answer` hands the tick loop to `_run_review_action_ticks` and the tick count to `_review_action_ticks`; the farm, combat and tick branches, the `finally` block and every RNG draw keep their order. `__init__.py` went from 5972 to 5849 lines; no public root name or signature changed.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty), before on this branch's base then after: `on_good_answer` 24/39/6.11 -> 9/11/2.0; `resolve_review_context_action` 18/21/5.17 -> 12/11/4.375; `_skill_availability_fingerprint` 16/13/1.0 -> `skill_availability_fingerprint` 10/9/1.0; `_catalog_requirement_item_names` 11/36/0.5 -> 4/6/0.5. ## 2026-09-24 — Equip and Unequip share one save path

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", root family. `on_equip_item` and `on_unequip_slot` each carried the same inner transition and the same live-save commit. The pure transition now lives in `core/equipment_change.py` (`equip_item_save_pure`, `unequip_slot_save_pure` and their shared `_save_after_move`: move, sync condition instances, reconcile the combat method only when the weapon changed), imported into the root under underscore names, so the pinned public root surface is unchanged. The root keeps one state-owning helper, `_commit_equipment_change`: Cooking gauntlets family items still go through `commit_direct_action` (undoable), every other item still saves through `_save_combat_state_update` when the weapon changed and through clear/update/`save_player_data` otherwise. Refusal messages, their order after the commit attempt, and the equip requirement check are unchanged. `__init__.py` went from 5849 to 5797 lines. No UI file changed; the Magic candidate `a8137bab` is untouched.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty), before on this branch's base `a6816946` (equal to the brief's `2021da75` figures) then after: `on_equip_item` 8 / 17 / 2.0 → 3 / 2 / 0.5; `on_unequip_slot` 8 / 16 / 2.0 → 3 / 1 / 1.0. Existing coverage of the other paths: `test_integration_smoke` (equip, requirement refusal, unequip, condition-instance sync, Cooking gauntlets undo both ways, weapon equip with combat reconcile). `tests/ui/test_encounter_transitions.py` also drives both handlers, but on this base `tests/ui/` has no `__init__.py`, so discovery never runs it (main has since moved it to `tests/ui_flows/`).

## 2026-09-24 — Pack yak Winter Storage and processing-pack scoping split out of the carried overlay

Branch `goal/carried-yak-storage-2026-09-24`, on `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`a6816946`). In `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_overlay.py`, `_try_pack_yak_winter_storage_overlay` keeps its name, signature and callers; it now asks `_winter_storage_selection` (policy threshold, scroll present, first eligible priority item, with `_winter_storage_candidate` as the per-item rule) and `_spend_winter_storage` (pack, bank scroll, special points, event log, XP). The processing branch of `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` hands its whole pack-scoped view of the bank (carried inputs capped at the pack, skilling potions hidden then carried potions re-scoped, unused Fletching material alternatives hidden) to `_scope_processing_inventory`, which returns the two hidden sets `_reconcile_processing_inventory` already takes. Order of dict writes, and so saved key order, is unchanged; neither part draws RNG.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty; limits 15 / 15 / 80), before → after: `_try_pack_yak_winter_storage_overlay` 34 / 20 / 7.84 → 9 / 5 / 2.5; `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` 152 / 205 / 10.83 → 135 / 164 / 10.77. Helpers: `_winter_storage_selection` 12 / 10 / 2.29, `_scope_processing_inventory` 11 / 10 / 2.67, `_spend_winter_storage` 9 / 3 / 3.38, `_winter_storage_candidate` 6 / 5 / 3.64, `_fletching_material_options` 5 / 7 / 0. The main overlay function still fails CCN and cognitive. `slop_gate.py` against a temporary index holding the file exited 0 with no output.

Equivalence: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 probe.py <tree> yak 200000` on a `git archive a6816946` snapshot and on this worktree printed 200000 byte-identical lines (return value plus `json.dumps` of the mutated player data; 1166 cases spent a scroll). `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 probe.py <worktree> scope 100000` compared a verbatim copy of the base scoping block with the new helper, dict order included: `scope cases 100000; mismatches 0; hidden alternatives 7285; hidden potions 60066; carried potion scoped 58701`.

## 2026-09-24 — Agility review handler split into lap steps

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", activity family, on top of `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23`. `handle_agility_answer` in `runtime/runtime_actions_activities_training.py` keeps its name, signature, registry entry and callers, and still calls the batch's aftermath helper `_resolve_agility_damage` unchanged. The rest of the review now reads as one step per helper: `_agility_entry_state` (the pre-review snapshot the Wilderness and Pit aftermath restores from), `_bank_agility_outputs`, `_award_agility_xp` (Agility XP, level check, cross-skill rewards), `_record_agility_result` (diary event and action record with arena tickets) and `_show_agility_feedback` (XP, status, death, auto-eat and safety-stop messages). Guards, the four-draw review roll, the save and the order of every callback are unchanged.

## 2026-09-24 — Combat burst end settlement and status tick split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", combat engine family. `apply_combat_burst_pure` keeps its name, signature, callers and place in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`; two responsibilities moved to the new `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_outcome.py`. `_combat_advance_status_tick` runs each tick's antipoison, status damage, eating after status damage, Void shifter escape and auto-flee, and returns why the burst stops (`died`, `void_shifter`, `auto_flee` or empty). `_combat_settle_burst_outcome` records kill counts, applies death-prayer damage and a safe or PvM death, clears an encounter with nothing to carry, and tells the caller whether to carry it. The carried-encounter build stays in the engine because it holds the reserved Magic state (spell factors, Spellbook Swap, Vengeance); the Dream block, every Magic branch and the Magic candidate `a8137bab` are untouched. Death prayers cap with `allow_automatic=False`, so their unreachable automatic-finisher increment was dropped (`tests/combat/test_death_prayer.py` pins that flag as `False`). Per-tick enemy turn resolution was not extracted: its damage path holds the Vengeance reflect, and it cannot fit one helper under the slop gate's per-function limit of 15, so it would become many small helpers.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty), before on this branch's base `a6816946` then after: `apply_combat_burst_pure` 299 / 561 / 17.79 → 280 / 532 / 17.89. Helpers: `_combat_settle_burst_outcome` 11 / 10 / 4.84, `_combat_advance_status_tick` 10 / 10 / 1.0. The engine went from 2696 to 2659 lines. The slop gate (`slop_gate.py` against a temporary copy of the index with both code files, `papercuts.md` and the results record staged) exited 0 with no output.

The cases include 11665 with a kill, 5350 with two or more kills, 9310 deaths, 1522 safe deaths, 3137 with death-prayer damage, 6336 auto-flees, 251 Void shifter escapes, 13861 carried and 24163 empty encounters, and 952 locked. The per-case output digests are byte-identical before and after (`4b8e0e82e77a7b59f8d2d0d58f49dd05ba73ac8b1cb73222a5f4f4ec6abdfa79`). Not done, found only through this work: a kill from the player's own hit respawns the enemy without resetting `retaliation_stopped`, while a kill during the enemy's turn resets it. ## 2026-09-24 — Root cleanup: the review-answer hook reads as its steps

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", root family, on top of `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`236ae8f5`). `on_answer_card` keeps its name, signature, `wrap(Reviewer._answerCard, on_answer_card, "around")` registration and `answer_wrapper` entry, and now lists the steps of one answered card: the undo snapshot and ignored-context XP, `_advance_clocks_for_answer` (shared review state, Livid Farm wish, shop and Wicked Hood day, daily challenges, Miscellania, Grand Exchange and Summoning ticks, each still in its own `try`), `_advance_effects_for_answer` (temporary effects, or disengaging a live-combat encounter on a failed answer), `_advance_farming_for_answer` (a queued Farming step or the farming clock; returns whether the step consumed the answer), `_dispatch_review_answer` (the Farming step, `on_good_answer` or the unproductive-activity handler owns the answer), then Anki's answer, `_commit_review_answer` (bankstanding switch, save, undo entry, deferred dialogs) and the surface refresh. The helpers stay in the root and resolve root names at call time, because tests replace root names such as `_show_exp` and `_runtime_action_context` there; no state moved and nothing is rebound against another module's globals. Two simplifications keep results exact: the failed live-combat branch reuses `live_combat` instead of calling `_cadence_kernel_enabled()` and `review_action_handler_key()` again, and the trailing `if context_xp > 0: _show_exp(context_xp)` is gone because every answered path already shows or hands off that XP and zeroes it (the review snapshot is never `None` for an answered card). `__init__.py` went from 5797 to 5786 lines; the root digest stays `74044844e6abb546bc2d895f337ec92c0afe66ddc9002931d65dc12256cd5f4e`.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty; limits 15 / 15 / 80), before on this branch's base then after: `on_answer_card` 41 / 47 / 4.45 → 7 / 5 / 1.0. Helpers: `_dispatch_review_answer` 9 / 12 / 2.6, `_advance_effects_for_answer` 9 / 9 / 2.33, `_advance_clocks_for_answer` 6 / 5 / 0.0, `_advance_farming_for_answer` 3 / 1 / 0.5, `_commit_review_answer` 2 / 1 / 0.0. `python3 ~/fleet/config/git/hooks/slop_gate.py` against a temporary index holding `__init__.py` exited 0 with no output.

Evidence root `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-root-answer-dispatch-2026-09-24/`; `artifact-sha256.txt` lists every raw artifact. Out-of-tree probe `answer_probe.py` (SHA-256 `3a529a28617d44bb110422f4fab23896104a5a54c0d5ca34b926f9fb9495bf73`) loads the add-on from a tree through the integration-smoke fakes and plays 3000 seeded scenarios of 8 cards each (the 17 review skills that have a default target, at that target, six Combat targets with and without a live encounter, all 42 activities with and without a session, idle, a queued Farming step in 30%, cadence on and off, review-action multipliers 1, 2, 5 and unset, unturned and unanswered cards, pre-awarded XP, eases 1 to 4, and Anki's answer raising in 5%). ## 2026-09-24 — Carried overlay bank-trip progression and no-pack handlers split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", carried family. Branch `goal/carried-overlay-next-2026-09-24` on `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`236ae8f5`). In `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_overlay.py`, `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` keeps its name, signature and callers. Bank-trip progression now takes two steps, split where a travel review restores the cadence admission: `_settle_bank_run_requests` (spend the manual request once a trip starts or arrives and the skilling request once one is active; on arrival restore summoning points and record the diary bank run) and `_spend_review_on_bank_trip` (save the trip, pack and churn of a review spent travelling, show its progress or arrival, run the diary callbacks, save). Handlers with no carried pack go to `_run_without_carried_overlay` (a bank run requested for the handler spends the review; one requested for its target arrives before the handler runs). Call order, dict writes, RNG draws and saves are unchanged. The Keep/Drop gathering settlement is untouched.

Boundaries not taken: the processing tick (scope, handler, Winter Storage rollback, reconciliation, deferred achievement and availability callbacks) and the first-trip pack priming each need 10 to 13 of the function's locals as parameters, because the rollback must run before reconciliation and before the deferred callbacks. A clean split of either needs one shared description of the target's pack first.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty; limits 15 / 15 / 80), before → after: `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` 135 / 164 / 10.77 → 119 / 146 / 10.55. Helpers: `_settle_bank_run_requests` 7 / 6 / 1.0, `_spend_review_on_bank_trip` 7 / 4 / 1.8, `_run_without_carried_overlay` 5 / 4 / 1.0. The file went from 877 to 921 lines. The main function still fails CCN and cognitive. `slop_gate.py` against a temporary index holding the file exited 0 with no output. `tools/module_surface.py --digest-only` is unchanged for the overlay (`0f749f48…`), `runtime/runtime_actions_carried.py` (`04437d32…`) and `runtime/runtime_actions.py` (`a84e797e…`).

Equivalence: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 probe.py <tree> 12000 8` drives `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` through 12000 seeded scenarios of 8 reviews each (all 15 carried handler keys plus 5 registered handlers without a pack and an unknown one; random bank, pack, churn, Custom and Drop modes, active and malformed trips, manual, skilling and handler bank-run requests, Pack yak, Wicked hood, Evil Tree and live cadence), logging every callback in order, every RNG draw and the player data JSON at each save and achievement check. A `git archive 236ae8f5` snapshot and this worktree wrote byte-identical output: 96000 reviews, SHA-256 `f38195d02edd0c958268f5f01bf03f6107d62688d2b2ba3dc42f3a5b99a695cf`, with 2644 handler-request arrivals, 1762 target-request arrivals, 6041 travel reviews in progress, 10658 arrivals with restock, 15201 rejected ticks, 1547 raised handler errors and 24629 RNG draws. Mutations, each in a throwaway snapshot copy deleted afterwards, changed the first 2000 scenarios: manual request kept on arrival 322 reviews, summoning not restored on a target-request arrival 675, travel churn not saved 4834. A progress floor of `max(0, …)` instead of `max(1, …)` changed nothing, because no probe state reports a travel review before one is spent.

## 2026-09-24 — Combat burst enemy turn and live-cadence rolls split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", combat engine family. `apply_combat_burst_pure` keeps its name, signature, callers and place in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`; two more responsibilities left it. The new `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_enemy_turn.py` holds the enemy's attack after it is chosen: `_combat_enemy_breathes_dragonfire` (dragonfire protection upkeep, potion and breath roll), `_combat_unprotected_special_damage` (a special the missing protective item lets through, with its prayer-blocked stat drains), `_combat_enemy_damage_after_prayers` (on-hit prayer drain, protection prayers, stacking specials, the lifepoint cap), `_combat_apply_vampyre_bite` (moved from the engine and now adds its own tallies) and `_combat_apply_redemption`. The new `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_live.py` holds the live-cadence rescaling of both sides' rolls: the former nested `_live_curse_adjustments` and `_live_prayer_factor`, plus `_combat_live_player_rolls` and `_combat_live_enemy_accuracy`. The engine now keeps its enemy-side result tallies in one `enemy_counts` dict. Still in the engine on purpose: the enemy attack selection and the ordinary hit roll, because `tests/test_brawling_gloves.py`, `tests/test_castle_wars.py` and `tests/combat/test_player_attack_rolls.py` patch `_combat_select_enemy_attack_pure` and `_combat_roll_damage_lifepoints` on the engine module; the stat-drain recompute through `_combat_player_attack_rolls`, which the last of those observes; the teleport and unequip specials, which end the burst; Vengeance and every other Magic branch; the Dream block. The Magic candidate `a8137bab` is untouched.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty; limits 15 / 15 / 80), before on this branch's base `236ae8f5` then after: `apply_combat_burst_pure` 280 / 532 / 17.89 → 255 / 448 / 18.48. Helpers: `_combat_apply_vampyre_bite` 8 / 8 / 3.0 → 10 / 11 / 4.84, `_combat_enemy_damage_after_prayers` 7 / 7 / 4.2, `_combat_enemy_breathes_dragonfire` 5 / 4 / 1.5, `_combat_unprotected_special_damage` 5 / 3 / 1.69, `_combat_apply_redemption` 4 / 2 / 3.8, `_combat_live_curse_adjustments` 5 / 4 / 0.67, `_combat_live_prayer_factor` 3 / 2 / 3.13, `_combat_live_player_rolls` 3 / 2 / 2.86, `_combat_live_enemy_accuracy` 1 / 0 / 1.5. The engine went from 2659 to 2434 lines. The slop gate (`slop_gate.py` against a temporary copy of the index with both new modules, the engine, `papercuts.md` and the results record staged) exited 0 with no output.

On an exported base tree and on this tree: `cases 49200, mismatches 0`, byte-identical digest files (`76d9da178f5f22615b480cab5e62884f172d9ae087e49eff092c25a3edddb2c3`). In-memory mutations, one per moved piece, changed: dragonfire roll 826, unprotected special 474, protection prayers ignored 3148, bite 65, Redemption 761, live player rolls 4338, live enemy accuracy 561 cases; no file was changed for them. Module-surface digests unchanged: engine `5cb6204b91995219447dd2fa7f700734014b234b99546907c6a5a7d16b66bf7b` (pinned), `logic_pure/logic_pure.py` `1441dccf792baea866ee7cddb7e583abbd83c12c0524f97416a5339d15e2b426`, `logic_pure/logic_pure_quest.py` `b66ca1b8c9dcc7f4f2dea7a20cace69b8bdd4922a3936384fbb73b7683754f68`.

## 2026-09-25 — Carried overlay processing tick and first-trip priming split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", carried family. Branch `goal/carried-overlay-third-2026-09-24` on `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`8bcc6ead`); it finishes the change a previous author left uncommitted when the owner paused the goal on 2026-09-24. In `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_overlay.py`, `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` keeps its name, signature and callers. `_describe_target_pack` reads the target's pack once, from the saved view, into a frozen `_TargetPack` (processing or not, Custom layout, bank, capacity, Agility casual layout, recipe, presence and potion requirements); its `repack()` replaces the five copies of the eight-argument `_repack_view_for_target` call, and a Winter Storage spend swaps in the new bank with `dataclasses.replace`, as the old `bank` reassignment did. That shared description lets the two boundaries the previous overlay slice could not take pass the pack instead of 10 to 13 locals: `_prime_first_trip` (the free first load of processing and Agility packs, and the saved Custom pack of a first Hunter trip) and `_run_processing_tick` (scope, handler, Winter Storage rollback, reconciliation, deferred achievement and availability callbacks, save). ## 2026-09-25 — One level-up popup for a multi-level gain

Check from the upstream-ideas card (`future-work-kanban.md`, owner accepted 2026-09-19). The runtime `level_up_check` in `__init__.py` only records a pending review XP event and delegates to `core.logic.level_up_check`, so both behave the same; the core loop called `show_level_up_dialog(skill, lvl - 1, lvl)` once per level. Real path driven: `on_claim_reward("the_elder_kiln_xp_lamp_1", "attack")` on a default save with The Elder Kiln complete, loaded as the full add-on with real offscreen Qt, gives 100,000 Attack XP (level 1 to 49). Before: 48 modal dialogs, `Level 1 → 2` through `Level 48 → 49`. After: one dialog, `Level 1 → 49`. The core check now sets the new level once, still writes one `"<Skill> advanced to level N"` review event per level, and then shows one popup `(skill, old_level, new_level)` behind the unchanged popup setting. Achievements, unlocks and the Combat-level popup are untouched.

Proof, evidence in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-level-up-dialog-2026-09-25/`:
- Before the fix: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt --select test_xp_lamp_crossing_many_levels_shows_one_level_up_dialog --expected-tests 1` → `Ran 1 test in 0.426s` / `FAILED (failures=1)`, `First list contains 47 additional elements.` (`qt-before-fix.log` `d9cf232c183c978c5705f13cdd6c10bc398783ed0d9dbb2a22709af7f3c854e9`).
- After: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" qt --select test_dialog_lifecycle --select test_level_up_check --expected-tests 15` → `Ran 15 tests in 0.744s` / `OK` (`qt-after-fix.log` `b358204c7ad884e1be351fde55c360c7faa03f698bc2ecc415209914b503f2d8`).
- `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_level_up_check --select test_fremennik_isles_lamps --select test_dispatcher_state --select test_xp_event_is_written_before_its_level_up_event --expected-tests 22` → `Ran 22 tests in 4.120s` / `OK` (`plain-after-fix.log` `5b86698231fcdcf8562909cd5468baf370d046c89eda7f6916588aa22bdab131`).

## 2026-09-25 — Carried overlay Wicked hood skip and live-cadence admission split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", carried family. Branch `goal/carried-overlay-fourth-2026-09-25` on `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`113ea200`). In `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_overlay.py`, `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` keeps its name, signature and callers. `_teleport_past_outbound_leg` takes the `_TargetPack` and spends a pending Wicked hood altar teleport on this target's active trip: it removes the outbound leg and, when nothing of the trip remains, restocks the pack with `pack.repack()` and returns that view, so the caller resets churn and the forced bank run as before. `_CadenceAdmission` replaces the closure and its four locals: `admit()` admits the review once (Magic and Prayer never wait on live cadence), and `restore_after_rejection()` puts back the cadence profiles unless a handler-owned scheduler advanced, and is still what `_run_processing_tick` receives as `restore_cadence`. Call order, dict writes, RNG draws, undo snapshots and saves are unchanged. The Keep/Drop gathering settlement is untouched.

Boundary not taken: the bank-run gate (trip due, trip price, waived trip, the pure gate, Evil Tree churn and the started trip's route). A single scratch helper for it, out of tree, still measured CCN 21 / cognitive 19 with nine parameters, even with Keep and Evil Tree overflow moved into `_TargetPack` and the route returned by `_initialize_due_bank_trip`. Splitting it into two helpers to pass the limits would be a split made only to pass the gate.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty; limits 15 / 15 / 80), before → after: `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` 87 / 104 / 9.53 → 79 / 87 / 9.0. New parts: `_teleport_past_outbound_leg` 7 / 4 / 2.75, `_CadenceAdmission.admit` 4 / 3 / 1.0, `_CadenceAdmission.restore_after_rejection` 4 / 4 / 1.0, `_CadenceAdmission.__init__` 1 / 0 / 0.5. The file went from 956 to 973 lines. The main function still fails CCN and cognitive. `slop_gate.py` against a temporary index holding the file exited 0 with no output. `tools/module_surface.py --digest-only` is unchanged for the overlay (`0f749f48…`), `runtime/runtime_actions_carried.py` (`04437d32…`) and `runtime/runtime_actions.py` (`a84e797e…`).

Mutations, each in a throwaway tree copy deleted afterwards, changed these many of all 96000 reviews: churn not reset when the teleport ends the trip 8, restocked view not returned 8, teleport not saved 23, a second admission not skipped 1485, Magic and Prayer admitted 66, cadence profiles not restored after a rejection 786. ## 2026-09-25 — Combat burst player hit and tick bookkeeping split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", combat engine family. `apply_combat_burst_pure` keeps its name, signature, callers and place in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`; two more non-Magic responsibilities left it. The player's hit: the new `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_player_turn.py` holds `_combat_land_player_hit` (the burst's damage multiplier, the finisher cap and melee recoil) and `_combat_soul_split_heal`, and both sides' landing and damage rolls now share `_combat_roll_hit`, which stays in the engine beside `_combat_roll_damage_lifepoints` because `tests/test_castle_wars.py:188` and `tests/combat/test_player_attack_rolls.py:71` patch that roller on the engine module. The tick loop's bookkeeping: `_combat_enemy_regeneration` and `_combat_regenerate_enemy` in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_enemy_turn.py` hold the enemy's regeneration set-up and per-tick healing, and whether a kill chains into the next spawn is decided once per burst as `stop_on_kill` instead of at both kill sites (`_combat_start_equipment_state` returns `(None, True)` when an enemy has no start equipment, so `not start_equipment_active` equals the old two-part test); `killed` is now `kills > 0`. Soul Split reads this tick's uptime in both cadences, which is unchanged outside live cadence because `tick_prayer_uptime` is then `prayer_uptime` itself. Still in the engine on purpose: firing ranged ammunition (its out-of-ammunition stop breaks the loop), rune use, borrowed power, spell effects, Magic XP, Vengeance, the carried-encounter build and the Dream block. The Magic candidate `a8137bab` is untouched.

## 2026-09-25 — Combat burst set-up of both sides and reward banking split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", combat engine family. `apply_combat_burst_pure` keeps its name, signature, callers and place in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`; two more non-Magic responsibilities left it. The set-up of both sides before the first tick: the new `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_setup.py` holds `_combat_enemy_start_lifepoints` (the enemy's maximum and current lifepoints, resumed from a carried encounter, and the lifepoints at which it stops fighting back and whether it already has), `_combat_prayer_request` (the selected prayers, the Saradomin halo's two-thirds drain on the prayers it covers, and the uptime asked for, read from the policy in live cadence) and `_combat_resume_attack_clocks` (both sides' attack clocks and the Curse attack count, resumed only by a carried live fight). In place, the enemy's defence roll is one expression: its three class branches differed only in the bonus they asked `_combat_defence_bonus_from_totals` for, and that function already picks the magic, ranged or melee style-type bonus from the player's class (`melee_style_type` is `None` outside melee); the multiplication order is unchanged. The banking after the tick loop: `_combat_bank_burst_rewards` in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_outcome.py` wears the equipped items for the ticks run and banks the drops and experience. The engine's use block no longer imports `CASTLE_WARS_HALO_DRAIN_PRAYERS`, `active_prayer_selection_pure` and `prayer_uptime_fraction_pure`; its re-export block still binds them, and `advance_equipped_condition_pure` and `EQUIPMENT_CONDITION_DATA` stay imported, so the pinned public surface is unchanged. ## 2026-09-25 — Combat burst enemy special damage and hit rebound split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", combat engine family. `apply_combat_burst_pure` keeps its name, signature, callers and place in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`; two more non-Magic parts of the enemy's turn moved to `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_enemy_turn.py`. `_combat_enemy_special_damage` chooses the damage of an attack that is not an ordinary hit (a wyvern's breath, dragonfire, or a special the missing protective item lets through), keeps their tallies and the breath event, and returns the damage (`None` for an ordinary hit), whether the attack ignores protection prayers, the player's attack delay and whether a stat was drained. `_combat_rebound_enemy_hit` applies the Deflect reflection, capped for the finisher, and then the vampyre bite; it reads the bite from the enemy, so the burst no longer derives `bite_spec` before the loop, and it subtracts the reflection unconditionally because `_combat_cap_damage_for_finisher` never returns a negative amount. Why the boundaries are safe: the ordinary hit roll (`_combat_roll_hit`, which calls `_combat_roll_damage_lifepoints`), the attack selection and the stat-drain recompute through `_combat_player_attack_rolls` stay in the burst, so the names tests patch on the engine module (`tests/test_brawling_gloves.py:216`, `tests/test_castle_wars.py:188`, `tests/combat/test_player_attack_rolls.py:71`) are still looked up there; the recompute now runs right after the special damage instead of inside its branch, which draws nothing from the RNG. Left in place, for the reasons the last slice gave: the prayer drain before the loop and in each live tick, the player's attack speed (its Magic branch reads the spell) and the result record, whose key order is saved data and which would need about forty inputs. Untouched: Vengeance (it reflects between the prayer cut and Deflect), every other Magic branch, the carried-encounter build and the Dream block. The Magic candidate `a8137bab` is untouched.

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead difficulty; limits 15 / 15 / 80), before on this branch's base `1613ede0` then after: `apply_combat_burst_pure` 198 / 332 / 16.52 → 185 / 303 / 16.63. The engine's use block no longer imports `_combat_dragonfire_damage_lifepoints`, `_combat_wyvern_breath_damage_event`, `_combat_apply_vampyre_bite` and `_combat_unprotected_special_damage`; nothing else imports them from the engine, and the pinned module surface is unchanged.

## 2026-09-26 — Combat burst target loadout and enemy attack preparation split out

Priority-matrix row "Remaining exposed-function cleanup", combat engine family. `apply_combat_burst_pure` keeps its name, signature, callers and place in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`; two more non-Magic parts moved out. `_combat_prepare_target_loadout`, in the new `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_loadout.py`, restores an empty custom pack and then applies the target's pinned preset or its overrides. `_combat_prepare_enemy_attack`, in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_enemy_turn.py`, turns the selected attack into its kind, the enemy it attacks as (with the selected style) and the teleport delay, and records the teleport special. The attack selection (patched by `test_brawling_gloves`) and the unequip gear transaction stay in the engine; so do the prayer drain, the player's attack speed with its Magic branch, the kill-and-respawn sites, the result record, the Magic code of candidate `a8137bab` and the Dream transaction.

Why a new module: putting the loadout helper in `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_setup.py` made the unchanged slop gate exit 1 with `slop-gate: new import cycles were introduced:`, because the model and supplies modules it needs share an import cycle with the engine and the set-up module was outside that cycle. The model (2859 lines) and supplies (1725 lines) files are over 1000 lines and must not grow. The new module imports only what the engine already imported, so the gate reads it as a split of the engine; on a temporary index with the three code files staged, `slop_gate.py` exited 0 with no output (`r2-slop-gate-code.txt`).

Metrics (lizard 1.24.0 CCN / complexipy 7.0.1 cognitive / radon 6.0.1 Halstead volume and difficulty), base `bb69fc1f` then after: `apply_combat_burst_pure` 185 / 303 / 4840.690777735002 / 16.63397129186603 → 177 / 290 / 4703.500046861354 / 17.0425. Helpers: `_combat_prepare_target_loadout` 8 / 10 / 30.0 / 1.0, `_combat_prepare_enemy_attack` 4 / 3 / 25.26619429851844 / 1.2, all under 15 / 15 / 80. The engine went from 2234 to 2211 lines; the enemy-turn module is 318 lines and the loadout module 28.

Proof (evidence directory `.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-combat-burst-sixth-2026-09-25/`, files prefixed `r2-` and `probe-r2-`). The fifth slice's `burst_probe.py` with custom-pack, existing-pack, target-override, pinned-preset and missing-preset setups and two more mutations (SHA-256 `371f085288ac2ff46021cc31796d23dda19473b97b1989126bb81cead19b67ea`) runs the frozen base engine `engine_before.py` (`4b738f5d8f2d83673b1760fa86067f532e46ad993de63b4292d32cbbfcaaf924`, equal to `git show bb69fc1f:logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_engine.py`) beside the tree's engine over 235 enemies, 25 setups, 9 option sets and 2 seeds, comparing full reprs, the complete RNG sequences and protocol-5 pickle bytes of returned and supplied data. `r2-after`: 105750 cases, 0 mismatches, digest file SHA-256 `7f99a4fafc482bfba5170d59f26115bdc079caeadf29a3f022f535be5911bc14`, byte-identical to the base-tree run; it reached 264 teleports, 197 unequips, 4230 cases per loadout setup and 23500 live-cadence cases. In-memory mutations, no source file changed: skipping target preparation gave 8284 mismatches (first `aberrant_spectre|custom_pack|default|0`); turning every prepared attack into an ordinary hit gave 882 (first `chaos_elemental|melee_strong|default|0`).

## 2026-09-26 — Target preview shows the prayer-boosted hit that combat lands

Defect from the read-only audit of main `d0904603` (finding 6). The target preview rebuilt the player's attack roll and max hit without prayers, while the combat burst rolls through `_combat_player_attack_rolls` with the prayer effects of the target's own setup (pinned preset, else per-target overrides) at the uptime left after prayer drain. A player at 60 Attack, Strength and Prayer with a Bronze sword, Ultimate Strength and Incredible Reflexes at full uptime against a Hill Giant saw `Your max hit: 79 LP` and 77.4% accuracy, but combat landed up to 89 LP (max hit 90) at 80.4%. With a Shortbow, Bronze arrows and Eagle Eye the preview said 83 LP at 78.7%; combat used 95 LP at 81.5%. `combat_target_preview_pure` now applies the same target loadout as the burst (`_combat_target_loadout`, shared), drinks its loaded potions on that copy with the burst's own `_combat_auto_drink_potions`, drains prayer over one ordinary review burst (`COMBAT_BURST_TICKS`, Saradomin halo multipliers shared through `_castle_wars_halo_drain_multipliers`) and feeds that uptime to the same helper for melee and ranged. So a Hill Giant override that turns prayer off shows 79 LP / 77.4%, an override or pinned preset that turns it on shows 90 LP / 80.4%, 1 prayer point at drain counter 59 shows the burst's 2% uptime (79 LP / 77.5%), and the same point with a loaded Prayer potion (4) shows the restored full uptime (90 LP / 80.4%, 82.6% with an Attack potion (4) too), each equal to the burst. Magic keeps its own unprayed projection, because Magic is owner-reserved (candidate `a8137bab` untouched). Turmoil's enemy-scaled boosts, curse ramps and live spell drains are added inside the fight, so the preview still leaves them out. No saved data, no UI code and no Dream code changed. `combat_target_preview_pure` measures lizard CCN 18 (was 20).

## 2026-09-26 — Combination runes respect the carried pack

The owner can make combination runes from the materials in the selected pack without spending materials left in the bank. Casual packs reserve one talisman per batch. Magic Imbue still waives that talisman for one action.

Failure modes identified before the fix: an empty or incomplete Custom pack spends bank materials; a partial pack exceeds its carried quantities; Casual packing reserves a talisman per essence instead of per batch; Magic Imbue fails to waive or restore the talisman requirement; bank refills or standard-rune behavior change.

The existing `_TargetPack` now gets Pure essence and the component rune as bulk materials, and the opposite talisman as a batch consumable unless Magic Imbue is active. Its existing packing, refill, scoping and reconciliation paths use those requirements. The pure crafting rules and saved-data format are unchanged. The settled rule is recorded in [the checked-in Runecrafting source audit](source-audits/runecrafting-2011scape-2026-06-17.md); no sources were fetched.

Proof on base `113ea200f477802b3e42793b20830d8ced99fef1`, branch `goal/combination-runecrafting-pack-2026-09-26`:

- After the fix: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_combination_runecrafting_pack. --expected-tests 7` → `Ran 7 tests in 0.005s`, `OK`, exit 0, no skips. ## 2026-09-26 — Minigame fights pay combat XP once

Audit finding on main `d0904603`, fixed on branch `goal/minigame-xp-once-2026-09-26` from `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`113ea200`); it finishes the change a previous author left uncommitted when the owner paused the goal. Players gain: Castle Wars, Pest Control and Soul Wars fights, and Barbarian Assault spellcasting, now give the XP the fight earned once, so damage dealt to Pest Control portals again pays its designed half-rate XP (`xp_multiplier` 0.5, `source-audits/pest-control-game-model-2026-07-18.md`) instead of full rate; a spell's base Magic XP per cast is not scaled by that multiplier and stays at its full value, as everywhere else in the engine. Cause: each review reducer returns the state the combat engine already credited (`apply_combat_burst_pure` adds `xp_rewards` to the save), and the four runtime handlers then added the receipt's `xp_rewards` a second time through a shared credit helper. The handlers now read the receipt only for level checks, the XP popup and telemetry; the helper is deleted. Barbarian Assault used the same pattern. Its Attacker front has `xp_multiplier` 0.0 (no pinned source, per `source-audits/barbarian-assault-role-model-2026-07-18.md`), which zeroes damage XP, so melee and ranged play there does not change; spellcasting still earns each spell's base Magic XP (`_combat_apply_magic_xp`, 5.5 per Wind Strike cast), which the handler paid twice. Open owner question, not changed here: that source audit says the front "records no Combat XP", but the engine still pays base Magic XP per cast there. The existing Castle Wars runtime test stubbed a reducer whose state lacked the XP its receipt reported; its fake state now carries that XP, as the real reducer's does, and still expects 12.0 Attack XP. No saved-data change.

Proof, evidence in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-minigame-xp-once-2026-09-26/`:

## 2026-09-26 — Prayer Off keeps Prayer potions

Defect from the read-only audit of main `d0904603` (finding 9): with Prayer set to Off, combat still drank a carried Prayer potion whenever a prayer was selected and points were missing. The player loses doses for prayers that never run. Off keeps the selected prayers so the player can turn them back on, and the potion auto-drink looked only at that selection and the missing points.

Fix: `_combat_auto_drink_potions` drinks a Prayer dose only when a prayer is selected and enabled and the uptime policy in `PRAYER_UPTIME_POLICIES` is above zero. This is the same policy table that the live combat cadence reads. It does not use `prayer_uptime_fraction_pure`, because that helper gives zero at zero points, which is exactly when a dose is needed. Design record: `memory-bank/ankiscape-skill-expansion.md` (uptime Off / 40% / 70% / 100%; mid-burst restore by auto-drunk Prayer potions). No source value, save change or migration. To keep the function's cyclomatic complexity at its base value of 16, the redundant `pop` of an empty dose was removed; the final loadout filter already drops empty doses.

Failure modes listed before the fix: Off spends a dose or changes points; turning prayers back on, or 40/70/100 at zero points, stops drinking; disabled prayers, an empty selection or full points drink; ordinary stat potions stop drinking; either combat cadence behaves differently. The two new tests in `tests/test_prayer.py` drive `apply_combat_burst_pure` through these states in both cadences.

Proof, evidence in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-prayer-off-potions-2026-09-26/`:
- Before the fix: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_prayer_off_preserves_potion_until_prayers_resume --select test_prayer_potions_restore_empty_points_only_when_configured_to_operate --expected-tests 2` → `Ran 2 tests in 0.004s` / `FAILED (failures=4)`: Prayer 46, 459/460 points, Thick Skin, Off reported 0.0 uptime and changed `{'Prayer potion (4)': 1}` to `{'Prayer potion (3)': 1}` in both cadences; Off at 0 points also drank (`red-opus.log` `64fcbd0682d914b4e336f1b26efeb5fc3a577800f4e482c842ade7e962611553`).
- After: the same runner, `plain`, the 23 selectors in `opus-selectors-2.txt` (every plain module that reads stat potions or prayer uptime, plus the two prayer deed tests of `test_achievement_diary_ledger_wiring`), `--expected-tests 957` → `Ran 957 tests in 83.658s` / `OK` (`plain-after-fix-opus.log` `143c952a76136e72185023f0dd998c7879d02548a92f359d9ad9ead9e6151411`).
- Slop gate on an alternate index: exit 0, empty output. lizard CCN of `_combat_auto_drink_potions` 16 → 16; complexipy 27 → 26; file length 1725 → 1725.

## 2026-09-26 — Quest combat preserves automatic fleeing

The player gains: a quest fight no longer switches automatic fleeing off for later ordinary fights. `apply_quest_combat_encounter_pure` turns `combat_auto_flee` off for the quest battle, and its restoration put back the target and encounter but not that flag, so the runtime saved `False` after any quest tick. Now it restores the player's prior value with the `True` default that the combat engine and the Fight Caves, TzHaar Fight Pit, Clan Wars and Crucible restorations use. An explicit `False` stays `False`. Saved data needs no migration. The intent was already settled by the code comment beside the override and by `source-audits/combat-quest-shared-engine-2026-06-24.md` (quest fights must not overwrite ordinary combat state); no period source value or owner ruling was needed.

Failure cases listed before the fix: a partial fight (no kill, no death) leaks the override; a kill or death leaks it; the runtime saves the leaked value; restoration turns an explicit `False` into `True`; an absent setting gets a value that differs from the engine default; the quest fight itself starts fleeing. `tests/combat/test_quest_auto_flee.py` starts Vampyre Slayer through normal progression and covers each case with real combat, the real `handle_quest_answer` save, and an ordinary combat tick after the quest tick. The override comment is one line shorter so `logic_pure/logic_pure_quest.py` stays at 1581 lines, the slop gate's limit for a file above 1000 lines.

Proof, evidence in `$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-quest-autoflee-restore-2026-09-26/`:
- Before the fix (base file): `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_quest_auto_flee. --expected-tests 3` → `Ran 3 tests in 0.026s` / `FAILED (failures=4)`, each `AssertionError: False != True` (subtests True and None of the partial fight, True of the death and of the runtime kill) (`opus-before.log`).
- Override removed as a mutation: the death subtest failed `AssertionError: False is not true` because the quest fight fled (`opus-no-override.log`); file restored, SHA-256 checked.
- After: the same runner with `--select` for `test_quest_auto_flee.`, `test_logic.`, `test_runtime_actions.` and the seven other modules that drive quest combat, `--expected-tests 526` → `Ran 526 tests in 10.382s` / `OK`, no skips (`opus-focused-final.log`).
- Memory Bank readers: `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` → `Ran 46 tests in 58.641s` / `OK` (`opus-memory-bank-readers.log`).

## 2026-09-26 — Ranged manual finishing blows

Ranged fighters can now finish a weakened Rockslug with the equipped bow and loaded Bag of salt, without losing arrows or the kill.

Cause and fix: `runtime/runtime_actions_combat.py` passed equipment metadata to its first requirement check but omitted it from the manual finisher. `logic_pure/logic_pure_combat_model.py` now accepts keyword-only `equipment_data` and forwards it to the same requirement check. The runtime supplies `EQUIPMENT_DATA`; positional callers and saved data remain compatible. No migration or gameplay rule changed.

Intent is settled in `memory-bank/source-audits/slayer-finishing-blows-2026-07-15.md` (Review-native mapping), the neighbouring burst call, and existing finisher tests. No sources were fetched. Failure risks listed before the fix: lost equipment context, bypassed restrictions, incorrect supply/kill settlement, a test that bypasses the runtime, and saved-data or unrelated combat changes.

## 2026-09-26 — Gear moves after a review undo in the right order

Player gain: putting on or taking off gear, or attaching a tool to the toolbelt, right after a review is its own Undo step, so the first Undo takes back the gear move and the next Undo takes back the review. Before, review Undo restored only the save keys the review changed: a review that mined one Copper ore followed by equipping a Bronze platebody left the platebody both worn and banked after Undo, unequipping made it vanish, and attaching an Iron pickaxe left it both on the toolbelt and in the bank. Found by a read-only audit of main `d0904603` (finding 1, "Review Undo duplicates subsequently equipped gear").

Cause and fix, `__init__.py`: `_commit_equipment_change` sent only Cooking gauntlets through `commit_direct_action` and saved every other equip and unequip directly, and `on_attach_tool` saved directly, so none registered an Anki undo entry. All three now run through `commit_direct_action`, the seam `design-thread-queue.md` §5.1 says every mutating handler uses, as their Bank-panel neighbours discard, consume, Rogue kit conversion and claims already do. A weapon change still runs the achievement check before the save and refreshes skill availability and the HUD after it. If Anki refuses the undo entry, nothing moves and the error now says Anki could not create an undo entry, instead of claiming the item is missing from the Bank or the slot is empty (the Mage Arena alignment handler's precedent). No save-format change.

Proof, evidence in `<git-common-dir>/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-undo-equip-duplicate-2026-09-26/`:
- Before the fix (base `__init__.py`, new tests): `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select GearMoveAfterReviewUndoTest --expected-tests 3` → `Ran 3 tests in 0.840s` / `FAILED (failures=3)`, each first Undo restoring the review instead of the gear move (`opus-red.log` `f50c4966d7c839653a7066b0f1f954a30352d0cf9b4877b1b3e07593f7dd7011`).
- Refused undo entry, before the reason fix: `--select test_a_refused_undo_entry_blames_anki_not_the_gear --expected-tests 1` → `Ran 1 test in 0.249s` / `FAILED (failures=3)`, each move blaming the gear, e.g. `'You need Bronze platebody in your bank to equip it.'` (`review-red.log` `7f27c93194e7ff5fc302513486b57624cdcd276f3476a4f6900854a637c4fd40`).
- After: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_equipment_change. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --select test_integration_smoke. --select test_cooking_gauntlets. --select test_equipment_instance_logic. --select test_encounter_lifecycle. --select EncounterMethodChangeIntegrationTest --select EncounterDirectActionTransitionTest --select test_dispatcher_state. --expected-tests 321` → `Ran 321 tests in 80.487s` / `OK` (`review-green.log` `bf810054f7ead08a90607297771c611b344da72c73fc3a68d460add479a629b6`).
- Memory Bank readers: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` → `Ran 46 tests in 62.896s` / `OK` (`opus-memory-bank.log` `db2ca695bef1d7b3b2974e94ec311920ad384ca6ac7d00518d71808c12bebc33`).

Not fixed, same defect class one step further out: `on_refill_combat_loadout`, `on_unload_combat_loadout`, `on_reclaim_death_office` and `on_do_bank_run_now` save through `_save_combat_state_update` without an undo entry, so any of them that changes the bank can meet the same review-Undo mismatch.

## 2026-09-26 — A partial urn keeps its charge while it waits in a grave

A partially filled training urn that the player carried into a Combat death now keeps its charge while it waits in the grave or in Death's Office, through any charging events and reloads in the meantime. Once the player reclaims it and carries it again, it continues to fill from the same point. Before this change, the first charge event or the next load cleared the charge; a carried spare runed urn then became the active urn, and the reclaimed urn could never fill again.

- Cause: `sanitize_urn_state_pure` in `data/urn_data.py` recognized the active urn only in the Bank, but death moves it into `combat_grave.items` or `death_office.items`. It runs on every load and at the start of every charge event. The sanitizer now also counts those two recovery lists, the same holders that the equipment-condition ledger uses (`systemPatterns.md`, per-copy condition holders and Combat death/reclaim). Charging still requires the urn in the selected target's carried pack. Save shape and config version are unchanged. No source value was needed, and no source was fetched.
- Failure modes listed before the change: a charge event or a reload during the wait clears the charge; a second death moves the urn into Death's Office and loses it; a carried spare takes over while the partial urn waits; an expired grave or a stale carried layout keeps a charge for an urn that is gone; malformed recovery lists keep a charge; the reclaimed urn cannot fill.
- Regression: `UrnRecoveryTest` in `tests/test_urns.py` charges an Impious urn to 50, applies a real PvM death (once for the grave, twice for Death's Office), sends a charge event with a carried spare, saves and reloads, reclaims by the grave walk or the paid Death's Office, and charges to 60. It also covers expiry with a stale layout and malformed or wrong-item recovery lists.
- Before the fix (HEAD `data/urn_data.py`, restored afterwards): `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select UrnRecoveryTest --expected-tests 3` exited 1: `Ran 3 tests in 0.004s`, `FAILED (failures=2)`, `AssertionError: True is not false` for both holders, because the waiting charge activated the spare.
- After the fix: the same command with `--select test_urns. --select test_prayer_passives. --select test_storage_pure. --select test_storage_migration_extra. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --expected-tests 178` exited 0: `Ran 178 tests in 19.424s`, `OK`.
- Readers of changed files: `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --select test_quest_action_proofs. --select test_worktree_guard. --expected-tests 79` exited 0: `Ran 79 tests in 60.892s`, `OK`.
- `audit_test_quality.py`: `4887 tests across 285 files`, `hard issues: 0`, `structural clone candidates: 31 groups`. `audit_item_reachability.py` exited 0: `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`. `audit_docs.py`, `worktree_guard.py audit` and `git diff --check` exited 0.
- The new `_owns_partial_urn` measures CCN 7 (lizard), cognitive complexity 8 (complexipy) and Halstead volume 41.51317942364757 (radon).
- Not fixed, recorded for the queue: `nonempty_urn_count_pure` counts only Bank urns toward the ten-urn cap, so urns in a grave do not count; Beast of Burden storage and dropped familiar cargo can hold items, but no production path moves items into a familiar, so an urn cannot reach them today.
- Evidence (`opus-*` logs): `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-urn-grave-progress-2026-09-26/`.

## 2026-09-26 — Interrupted legacy save repair already keeps the newer copy

Audit finding 6 (interrupted legacy TEXT repair loads the older primary and ignores the newer recovery copy) does not reproduce in real Anki, so the loader is unchanged. Anki 25.09.4 `get_config` returns its default for a TEXT-backed `ankiscape_player_data` row in the same session and after reopening (`review-text-row-probe.txt` `87d07cb53d4716dc1ba872f8bd3ba17f625d169cd9bb1a1ac4f02ef006276490`: `typeof text`, `same-session get_config None`, `reopen get_config None`, `set_config DBError ... InvalidColumnType(0, "val", Text)`). The existing `if not loaded:` fallback therefore already selects recovery after a repair that stopped before the delete. The audit's 7-versus-99 result came only from `_LegacyTextCollection`, whose `get_config` returns the TEXT primary; the correction is recorded in `memory-bank/source-audits/circus-horizontal-implementation-2026-07-21.md`.

Players gain no behaviour change. What stays is regression coverage in `tests/test_storage_runtime.py` `InterruptedLegacyRepairTest`, five tests on real temporary Anki collections with close/reopen: interruption before the delete, interruption after the delete, later saves while recovery cleanup fails, a failed recovery write, and a repaired BLOB primary that is newer than leftover recovery. All five pass on the unchanged base loader.

Owner question, not fixed here: a legacy TEXT primary with no recovery key loads as default data, because Anki cannot read it, and the next save's repair writes those defaults to recovery, deletes the TEXT row and recreates the primary from defaults, so the old progress is lost. One possible shape is to read the TEXT primary through `mw.col.db.scalar` when `get_config` returns nothing; recovery must then win over a readable TEXT primary. The 2026-07-21 pre-repair copy `/tmp/ankiscape-player-config-before-type-repair-2026-07-21.anki2` no longer exists, so whether the owner's own profile lost progress then is unverified.

Proof, evidence in `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-legacy-recovery-copy-2026-09-26/`:

## 2026-09-26 — Large stackable supplies no longer stall bank packing

When fletching headless arrows or anything using only stackable supplies, answering a card no longer freezes for a few seconds even with millions in the bank; you see the same quick response and end up with the same pack.
- Settled rules: [carried inventory design](inventory-carry-model-design-2026-07-07.md) requires whole recipe batches within bank stock and slot limits; [computation budget](simulation-computation-budget-contract-2026-07-17.md) requires bounded review work.
- Failure modes considered before code: partial batches, overdrawn stock, excess slots, lost bag capacity, changed mixed recipes, and a test that misses the real review refill.
- The packer uses its existing complete-batch limit as the step for fully stackable recipes, after the existing slot check; mixed recipes keep their existing step. No saved fields, migration, UI, Magic candidate or Dream transaction changed.
- The regression starts from a default save, empties a pack through the real Fletching handler, then refills through the review wrapper with 15,000,000 Arrow shafts and 15,000,000 Feathers. No production function is mocked. It checks the two-slot pack, consumption, output and a slot-check budget of 28.

## 2026-09-26 — Casual Cooking keeps useful gloves when gauntlets cannot stop burns

The player keeps Brawling gloves (cooking) and their extra XP when Cooking gauntlets would not stop any burns: picking the fish, setting its Gauntlets choice back to Casual, and every review now agree. The settled rule is `gap-grill-decisions-2026-07-10.md:1200-1203`: Casual equips an owned pair when it helps; Advanced keeps the saved choice. No sources were fetched.

Failure cases listed before implementation: an unnecessary swap at guaranteed success, suppressing useful burn protection, confusing live and legacy odds, failing to restore displaced gloves, overriding explicit or manually worn gear, breaking review undo, and the target and policy direct actions disagreeing with the review.

`cooking_gauntlets_beneficial_pure` in `logic/cooking_gauntlets_logic.py` is the one place that decides: an explicit Cooking choice on a threshold recipe, or a higher success chance with gauntlets than without under the active roll (the source roll on a live embedded-probability route, the legacy curve otherwise) at the effective roll level. The review handler and both main-menu direct actions pass its answer to the unchanged reconciler; the root module imports it inside `_on_main_menu`, so the pinned root surface does not move. Save data and UI are unchanged.

## 2026-09-26 — A processing action that fails midway no longer shrinks the bank to the pack

Player gain: when something goes wrong partway through a Firemaking, Crafting, Smithing or other carried processing action, the bank keeps everything it really holds, minus only what that action used. Before, the live bank was left as the pack-capped working view, so a 100-log bank read 27 Logs and 1 Ashes after one burn, and the next save could have written that. Found by the read-only GPT-6 Astra audit of main `d0904603` (probe only; no save was written, so this was a risk, not an observed disk loss). Branch `goal/processing-exception-inventory-2026-09-26` on `c965b181`.

Cause: `_run_processing_tick` in `runtime/runtime_actions_carried_overlay.py` swaps `player_data["inventory"]` for the capped view, calls the handler, and reconciled only on a normal return; `handle_firemaking_answer` writes the new inventory before `award_skill_exp`, so a raising XP callback left the capped view live. Fix: the handler call sits in `try`/`finally`, and the existing `_reconcile_processing_inventory` settles the real bank in the `finally`, as the file's own comment requires ("a suppressed save can never leave the capped inventory as the last thing written"). The exception still propagates, and a raise skips all post-handler work: pack decrement, churn, bank-run state, deferred callbacks and save. A handler that returns False is unchanged: unless Winter storage was used, it skips the pack, churn and bank-run writes but still runs the requested deferred callbacks and saves the settled bank; after Winter storage, the rejection restores and saves the pre-storage snapshot and returns without running those callbacks. The pack view written before the handler stays as it was, so it still counts the consumed input (see Not fixed). `_run_processing_tick` lizard CCN 9 → 10; file 973 → 975 lines; `slop_gate.py` on a temporary index exited 0 with no output.

Proof, evidence in `<git-common-dir>/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-processing-exception-inventory-2026-09-26/`:
- Before the fix: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_a_raising_handler_leaves_the_real_bank_not_the_capped_pack --expected-tests 1` → `Ran 1 test in 0.001s`, `FAILED (failures=1)`, `AssertionError: {'Logs': 27, 'Ashes': 1} != {'Logs': 99, 'Ashes': 1}` (`red-plain.log` `73c22e1a837e9b847010d839f6493ca91af4cd671b47eae4452f23a6ae851fdd`).
- After: `--select test_carried_processing_reconciliation. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --select test_runtime_actions. --select test_urns. --select test_cadence_live. --select test_skilling_boost_logic. --select test_woodcutting_split. --expected-tests 316` → `Ran 316 tests in 17.351s`, `OK` (`green-plain.log` `cef2088703095affe33ff6641e8823424ab9a104a28c407ed7b08e2d7ff14ef1`).
- Memory Bank readers and overlay neighbours: `--select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --select test_carried_new_bank_trip. --select test_bank_run_request_target. --select test_carried_keep_settlement. --expected-tests 53` → `Ran 53 tests in 59.959s`, `OK` (`plain-memory-bank-and-overlay.log` `436e441fa404cdee1558be2951bc5cced5fc99a5455cf806917defbba817d3b3`).
- `audit_test_quality.py` `4885 tests across 285 files`, `hard issues: 0`, `structural clone candidates: 31 groups`; `audit_item_reachability.py` exit 0, `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`, equal to the baseline; `audit_docs.py`, `worktree_guard.py audit` and `git diff --check` in `audit-docs.txt`, `worktree-audit.txt`, `diff-check.txt`. Full lanes not run here; the promotion gate runs them on the merged tree.

Not fixed (second level): on a raise the pack itself is not decremented, so the trip carries one more use than the bank lost; the audit's proposed `execute_processing_action` transaction module was not built.

## 2026-09-26 — A failed reward, or a card answer that errors while Anki is still on the answer side, goes back to where it was

Player-visible fix from the read-only Astra audit of main `d0904603` (findings 1 and 2), on `goal/reward-transaction-failure-2026-09-26` from `promote/runtime-batch-2026-09-23` (`113ea200`); it finishes the change a previous Opus author left uncommitted at 14:10. The design records say one answer or menu action is one atomic transaction (`simulation-time-boundary-2026-07-16.md`, `review-hud-context-actions-contract-2026-07-18.md`) and that gameplay never runs ahead of the card schedule (`systemPatterns.md`, review rewards paired with Anki undo). Two paths broke that:

- `commit_direct_action` installed the new save and its undo snapshot, then ran `after_apply` and the save. If either raised, memory kept a half-paid reward (the Troll Invasion book gone and its XP granted) while the saved profile still held the book. Now a raise restores memory, the save, the RNG, the skill, the queued achievement popups and the Bankstanding notice from the snapshot and re-raises. The snapshot stays on the stack because Anki's custom undo entry already exists; undoing it restores the same state, so it can never take back the earlier action. Anki's Undo/Redo menu is refreshed on that failure path too, so the kept entry shows as Undo.
- `on_answer_card` saved and remembered the paid answer in a `finally`, so when Anki's answer method raised (Anki leaves the card on its answer side: `aqt/reviewer.py` 538-562 in the pinned `.venv-qt` sets `state = "transition"` only after `build_answer` succeeds), the game kept the burnt log and XP for a card Anki never counted, and the retry paid nothing. Now, when the reviewer is still on its answer side, the rejected answer rolls back the same way and re-arms the reviewer flags, so the same press is paid once when Anki accepts it. Once Anki has moved to `transition`, the hook commits as before and does not re-arm, because Anki ignores later presses there; this slice promises no rollback for a failure past that point. The accepted path is unchanged.

`_capture_player_data_snapshot` also records the queued popup count and the pending notice; neither is saved, so saved data is unchanged. `__init__.py` stays at 5786 lines because eight stale "moved to ui.py"/"removed legacy" breadcrumb comments were deleted (the slop gate forbids growth above 1000 lines).

Proof (evidence in `<git-common-dir>/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-reward-transaction-failure-2026-09-26/`):
- Before the fix (base `__init__.py`, new tests): `run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_rejected_answer_keeps_the_final_log_for_one_paid_retry --select test_rejected_answer_out_of_logs_leaves_no_stale_bankstanding_notice --select test_failed_reward_book_keeps_the_book_for_a_retry_and_a_harmless_undo --expected-tests 3` → `Ran 3 tests in 0.822s`, `FAILED (failures=4)`: both reward subtests lost `'Troll Invasion reward book': 1` from memory, the final-log answer kept `'Logs': 0, 'Ashes': 1`, and the out-of-logs answer left `'Utilities' != 'Firemaking'`.
- Mutants, each restored at once (`cmp` identical): no notice restore → `Lists differ: ['You need 1 Logs to burn Logs.'] != []`; no popup truncation → the queued popup survived; both `Ran 2 tests`, `FAILED (failures=1)`.
- After: `plain --select test_dispatcher_state. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --select test_integration_smoke. --expected-tests 276` → `Ran 276 tests in 85.322s`, `OK`.
- `qt --select test_dispatcher_state. --expected-tests 16` → `Ran 16 tests in 4.178s`, `OK`.
- After the Memory Bank edits: `plain --select test_dispatcher_state. --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 62` → `Ran 62 tests in 60.413s`, `OK`. Full lanes not run here; the promotion gate runs them on the merged tree.
- Review fixes (answer side, Undo menu): the rollback now also requires `self.state == "answer"`, and `commit_direct_action` refreshes `mw.update_undo_actions()` in a `finally` so a failed reward's kept entry reaches Anki's menu. Red, both fixes reverted in one mutant and restored at once (`cmp` identical): `plain --select test_answer_failing_after_anki_leaves_the_answer_side_is_not_re_armed --select test_failed_reward_book_keeps_the_book_for_a_retry_and_a_harmless_undo --expected-tests 2` → `Ran 2 tests in 0.448s`, `FAILED (failures=3)`: `AssertionError: 1 != 0` (the log came back after Anki left the answer side) and twice `AssertionError: 1 not greater than 1` (no menu refresh). Green: `plain --select test_dispatcher_state. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --select test_integration_smoke. --expected-tests 277` → `Ran 277 tests in 94.952s`, `OK`; `qt --select test_dispatcher_state. --expected-tests 17` → `Ran 17 tests in 4.344s`, `OK`; Memory Bank readers `plain --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 46` → `Ran 46 tests in 60.213s`, `OK`.
- Audits: `audit_docs.py` `Audited 871 Markdown and 452 HTML files.` / `Documentation audit passed.`; `audit_test_quality.py` `4888 tests across 285 files`, `hard issues: 0`; `audit_item_reachability.py` exit 0, `1680 actions; 2350 reachable items; 163 orphan inputs; 249 blocked actions`, equal to the baseline; `worktree_guard.py audit` `Worktree layout safe`; `git diff --check` clean; `slop_gate.py` on a temporary index exit 0, no output.
- Review fix (partial save): the real save writes the profile row, then the `ankiscape_current_skill` row. If that second row kept failing, the rollback's own skill-key write raised before it rewrote the profile, so the saved profile kept the spent book while memory had it back. `_restore_direct_action_snapshot` now restores the RNG and saves the profile before the skill-key write and the menu update. Red, the old order restored and the fixed file put back at once (`cmp` identical): `plain --select test_reward_whose_skill_key_stays_locked_keeps_the_stored_book --expected-tests 1` → `Ran 1 test in 0.266s`, `FAILED (failures=1)`, the stored profile lost `'Troll Invasion reward book': 1` (test line 475). Green: `plain --select test_dispatcher_state. --select test_direct_action_undo_seam. --select test_integration_smoke. --expected-tests 278` → `Ran 278 tests in 79.506s`, `OK`; `qt --select test_dispatcher_state. --expected-tests 18` → `Ran 18 tests in 4.908s`, `OK`; the 28 other plain modules that name the direct-action undo stack → `--expected-tests 109`, `Ran 109 tests in 30.927s`, `OK`; `qt --select test_activity_one_click. --select test_encounter_transitions. --expected-tests 17` → `Ran 17 tests in 4.234s`, `OK`; `audit_test_quality.py` `4889 tests across 285 files`, `hard issues: 0`; reachability unchanged at the baseline.

Not fixed, found while tracing (second level): `_commit_review_answer` saves before it remembers the answer's undo snapshot, so a save error after Anki accepts an answer leaves no snapshot and that card's Undo would pop the previous card's; a failure inside Anki's background answer operation cannot reach this hook at all; `commit_direct_action` does not put the RNG back when `apply_change` itself raises; a `reviewer_will_answer_card` veto (`proceed=False`) returns normally, so the game still pays for that press (pre-existing); Anki sets `transition` before `answer_card(...).run_in_background(...)`, so if that launch raises synchronously the card is never scheduled yet the game keeps the payment (pre-existing; the transition-side test pins only that the press is not re-armed).

## 2026-09-26 — Purchases no longer postpone shop restocking

A sold-out shop item now returns on schedule while the player keeps trading in that shop. Before, every purchase or sale reset the unfinished part of each row's restock wait, so buying one Fishing bait every 25 reviews kept a sold-out Small fishing net at zero indefinitely. Branch `goal/shop-restock-time-2026-09-26`, based on `113ea200f477802b3e42793b20830d8ced99fef1`.

Settled design: `npc-shop-design-grill-2026-06-24.md` decisions 3, 14, 15 and 44 require persistent per-row progress, lazy group evaluation and compact default stock. No source harvest or new period value was needed.
Failure risks listed before implementation: discarded partial intervals, duplicate replenishment, mixed row intervals, excess/temporary stock decay, save/reload or undo loss, and banking time at full stock or across the default.
`logic/shop/state.py` keeps each row's unfinished interval as `restock_progress` when a transaction checkpoints the group clock. One setter clears it whenever the quantity reaches or crosses the default, from restocking, a purchase or a sale, so time earned while short never shortens overstock decay and the reverse. Rows without the value load as zero; no schema version or migration changed.
`tests/shops/test_shop_restock.py` drives real purchases, sales, review-clock advancement, JSON round trips, views and undo: the reported five-net case with bait every 25 reviews against an uninterrupted control, mixed intervals, overstock and temporary decay, undo, full stock, and both crossings of the default.

Red before the fix: `--select TestShopRestock.test_purchases_preserve_accumulated_restock_time --expected-tests 1` returned `AssertionError: 0 != 1`, `FAILED (failures=1)`, exit 1 (`red.log`). Sell-across-default regression with the setter absent: `AssertionError: 5 != 6`, exit 1 (`review-f1-red.log`). Buy-across-default regression with only the buy call site reverted: `AssertionError: 1 != 0`, exit 1 (`review-f1-buy-red.log`); the line was restored before any further step.
Green: `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 tools/run_remote_tests.py "$PWD" plain --select test_shop_restock. --select test_shop_logic. --select test_module_boundaries. --select test_vendor_actions. --select test_early_quest_item_routes. --select test_quest_action_proofs. --select test_rune_shards. --select test_world_clock_no_input_mutation. --select test_first_wave_digests --select test_promotion_gate. --select test_memory_bank_queue_packet. --expected-tests 144` returned `Ran 144 tests`, `OK`, exit 0, zero skips (`review-final.log`). `test_ranging_guild` and `test_cape_tradeability` also trade in shops but carry Qt-only or Anki-runtime skips, so they wait for the promotion gate's Qt lane.
